patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

teṉcoṟ kaṭṭurikaḷ

1
vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām Turanian, Semitic, Aryan eṉa mup perum pirivukaḷā yaṭaṅkumeṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟiṉām. avaṟṟuḷ  āriyak kaviyil Teutonic kiḷikkut talimiyāṉatu vaṭamoḻi yeṉṉum camaṟkirutam; ēṉiya German, Dutch, English mutaliyaṉa. 
mēṉāṭṭu moḻiyārāyccip pulavar, ilakkaṇa oṟṟumiyālum ilakkiyac coṟkaḷiṉ oṟṟumiyālum vaṭamoḻiyic cerumaṉ moḻikku iṉamākak kaṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, intiya āriyarum ceru kṉiyarumxruflattilxrētkuppiṉarāyxrēyiṭattil(mēlāciyā)tḻntirukkanvaṇṭum.rÇ¤âu nūlkaḷellām perumpālum kiṟittu vukkup  piṟpaṭṭaṉavātaliṉ, avarkku muṟpaṭṭa varalāṟukaḷi yellām uṇartaṟku moḻinūlē ciṟanta karuviyākiṉṟatu. oru vakuppāri iṉṉā reṉṟu uṇartaṟku avaratu pēcciṉum ciṟanta karuvi piṟitoṉṟilli.
nilattiṟ kiṭantami kālkāṭṭuṅ kāṭṭuṅ
kulattiṟ piṟantārvāyc col.	(kuṟaḷ. 959)
ākavē, intiya āriyar cerumāṉiyarkku iṉameṉṟum avar pēciya moḻiyum cerumaṉukku iṉameṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum. ati avviru moḻikaṭkum iṉṟiyamiyāta cila potuc coṟkaḷāṟ kāṭṭutum. iṅkilīṣceruma‹moḻiyiṉiṉṟu«piṟantatātaliṉ,kīḻvaru«iṅkilīZcoṟkaḷellā«cerumaṉeṉwteḷika. (Jutes, Saxons, Angles eṉṟa mūvakic cerumāṉiya vakuppiṉarē ki.mu. 449-l cerumaṉiyiṉiṉṟum pirittāṉiyāvukkup pōy iṅkilīṣ moḻiyi vaḻaṅkiṉavar.)
English	camaṟkirutam	English 	camaṟkirutam
iṉi ilakkaṇa oṟṟumikaḷāvaṉa: 
1. mey moḻi mutalātal
2. orumi irumi paṉmi eṉa eṇ mūṉṟātal
3. peyareccaṅkaḷ pāl eṇ vēṟṟumikaḷik kāṭṭal
4. ilakkaṇap pāl (grammatical gender)
5. viṉimuṟṟukaḷ pāl kāṭṭāmi
6. payaṉili viṉiccollāyē yiruttal 
ivi pōṉṟavi iṉṉum ettuṇiyō uḷa. mukkiyamāṉavi maṭṭum iṅku ctāraṇamākakFṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. 
MriyamvṉṉumFṟiyīṭuKjṉmutalāÇyakḻikaḷešlāva‰ṟiṟkumgtuvāyvaHṅkiṟṟu.pi‹ò Teutonic vakuppukku maṭṭum vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉpu intiya āriya moḻiyākiya camaṟkirutattiṟkē ciṟappāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. intiya āriyar nāl vakuppārāyiṉum, avarkaḷukkuḷ pirāmaṇar maṭṭum ik kuṟiyīṭṭit tamakkum tam moḻikkum variyaṟuttukkoṇṭaṉar.
āriya vakuppu moḻikaḷuḷ Latin, Greek eṉṟa iraṇṭum muṟpaṭṭavi. Teutonic moḻikaḷ avaṟṟiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavi. airōppiya aracukaṭkellām muntiya kirēkka carittiram ki.mu. 3000ām āṇṭi ṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ākavē, camaṟkiruta  āriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉatum atē kālamāyttā ṉirukkavēṇṭum. 
ulakil mutaṉmutal uṇṭāṉatu tamiḻ. tamiḻar (ki.mu. 8000) kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟum vaṭakkē pōyc ciṉṉa āciyāvil kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpatu pāpilōṉiyak kuki veṭṭālum kuḻi veṭṭālum teriya varukiṉṟatu. aṅkētāṉ tamiḻ cēmiyak (Semitic) kiḷiyākat tirintatu. epirēyam (Hebrew), arapi (Arabic) mutaliyaṉa cēmiyak kiḷi moḻikaḷ. avaṟṟiyaṭuttu Latin, Greek mutaliya āriyak kiḷi moḻikaḷ piṟantaṉa. oru tāymoḻiyiṉiṉṟum oru kiḷimoḻi uṇṭāyiruppiṉ ataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta mutaṟ coṟkaḷellām tāymoḻiyā yirukkum. ēṉiyavellām ataṟkuc contamāṉa ākkac coṟkaḷē. avaṟṟuḷ oru pakuti piṟamoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉkoṇṭa ticiccoṟkaḷā yirukkum. oru moḻiyiṉ ākkaccoṟ perukkattiṟkum curukkattiṟkum kāraṇam am moḻiyi vaḻaṅkum makkaḷaṟivē yākum. 
āriyak kiḷikaḷil oṉṟākiya Teutonic eṉpatu Greko-Latin eṉṟa peruṅkiḷiyiṉiṉṟum kiḷittapoḻutē (atāvatu camaṟkiruta   āriyar intiyāvukku varumuṉṉamē) atil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa  tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kalantiruntaṉa. āriyarkaḷ intiyāvukku vantu tamiḻa rōṭu (ki.mu. 2500) kalantapiṉ eṇṇiṟanta teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalantaṉa. āriyar varumuṉṉum vantapoḻutum vaṭintiyāvil vaḻaṅkivantavi yellām koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷē. 
āriya varukikku muṉ camaṟkirutattil allatu Teutonic moḻikaḷiṟ kalantirunta teṉcoṟkaḷiṟ cila:

 				vesture		vastram
camaṟkirutattiṟku mutaṟkāviyam irāmāyaṇam. irāmar iruntatu iṭiccaṅka kālam. ākavē, tolkāppiyamum akattiyamum irāmāyaṇattiṟku muṟpaṭṭaṉavām. vaṭamoḻikku mutal ilakkaṇam pāṇiṉīyam. pāṇiṉi pāratattiṟkum piṟpaṭṭavar. pāratakālam 1000 B.C.; irāmāyaṇa kālamō 2000 B.C.; ākavē, pāṇiṉīyam tolkāppiyat tiṟkuk kuṟintatu āyiram āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. āriya varukikku muṉṉamē tamiḻar muttamiḻilum palkalikaḷilum tērntirun taṉar. akattiyam vaḻinūlē yaṉṟi mutaṉūlākātu. itaṉ virivi yellām ematu mutaṟṟāymoḻiyiṟ (The Classica Prima) kaṇṭukoḷka. 
iṉi, vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷāvaṉa: 
matam 
	mati + am = matam.
tēy + u = tēyvu - tēvu - tē - tī.
tēyvu + am - tēyvam - teyvam Gk. Theos.
tēyvam - tēvam - tēvaṉ. 
teyvam eṉpatu mutalāvatu neruppiyum piṉpu civattiyuṅ kuṟikkum. (tiruvācaka virivuri kāṇka.) 
civa + m = civam - civaṉ - civi.
uru + tiram = uruttiram - uruttiraṉ - rutraṉ = ciṉaṉ. 
uruttal = ciṉattal, tiram toḻiṟpeyar vikuti. 
ari + am = aram - araṉ.
ō - ōm - ōṅkāram or ōkāram. 
ōveṉpatu mūla volikkuṟippu. ol, oli, ōlam, ōci, ōti mutaliya ōcipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷellām okara ōkāra mutaṉmoḻikaḷātal kāṇka. rī eṉpatu rīṅkāramāṉāṟ pōla ō eṉpatu ōṅkāramāyiṟṟu. 
uru + tiram + ākkam = uruttirākkam - ruttirākṣam (uruttirākka viḷakkaṅ kāṇka.) 
vi + pūti = vipūti. puḻuti - pūti; vi, upacarkkam.
āṇavam: āḷ - āṇ - āṇmi - āṇavam.
māy + ai = māyi: ai. - to. pe.vi.
kāmi + am = kāmiyam, kām + iyam  eṉṟum pirikkalām.
iyam oru toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
kām + am = kāmam. 
kām + ar = kāmar. 
kumaraṉ, kumari - kommi(tiraṭci) eṉṉum paṇpiṉaṭiyākap  piṟantavi. cf. virgin Gk. orago - to swell. cēy (Zeus), murukaṉ, vēlaṉ eṉpaṉa kumaraṟku maṟupeyarkaḷ. 
caṇṭiki - caḷ - caḷḷi - caṇṭi - caṇṭaṉ - caṇṭi -caṇṭiki. 
kāḷi : kaḷ - kaḷḷam - kāḷam - kāḷi.
             - kaḷam - kaḷaṅkam = karuppu. 
ciṉaṉ : ciṉa + am = ciṉam - ciṉaṉ.
viṣṇu : viṇ - viṇṭu. viṇṇippōl nīlamāyum eṅkum   niṟintumiruppavaṉ. 
māyōṉ : mā - māy - māyōṉ.  mā = karuppu.
kiruṣṇaṉ: kaḷ - kaḷḷaṉ - kaṇṇaṉ. 
māyōṉ, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ kiruṣṇāvatārattiṟ putitāy uṇṭāṉaviyalla. ataṟku muntiyē vaḻaṅkiṉavi. māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟi yulakamum eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu kāṇka. 
araṅkaṉ:- araṅkam - araṅkaṉ. araṅkam = āṟṟiṭikkuṟi.
irāmaṉ:- irāmam - irāmaṉ. 
irāmam eṉpatu tirumaṇkāppu. irāmam = irēki vari. irāmak karumpu eṉṉum vaḻakku nōkkuka. vaṭanāṭṭil itit tavaṟāka nāmam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. irāmar oru viṇava paktar. irāmarukku muṉpē māl vaḻipāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntatu. 
iraṇṭu, alampu, ilakku, ulāttu mutaliya coṟkaḷ uyirmutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkuvatupōla araṅkaṉ irāmaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷum uyir mutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nārāyaṇaṉ: nāram + ayaṉaṉ.
varuṇaṉ: vār + aṇam - vāraṇam - vāraṇaṉ - varuṇaṉ.
vār + i = vāri. vār + iti = vāriti.
ātmā: āṉ + mā = āṉmā. āṉ = pacu. mā perumip poruḷ  vikuti. māṉ eṉpatu īṟu kuṟintu niṉṟateṉiṉum okkum. Gk. animos. vaṭamoḻiyil ātmā carīrattik kuṟikkum.
naraṉ: nari + aṉ = naraṉ = narippavaṉ.
pati: patikkut talivaṉ;  iṭavāku peyar. pati = nakar, vīṭu.
pacu: pācattāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu pacu.

cf. dungeon, from dung.

ulattal = aḻital.
nīr + am = nīram - nāram = nār + am.

c.f. Aurora Borealis, the northern light.

``kaṇkāl puṟamakam uḷḷuḻi kīḻmēl

iṉi, akkā, attā, āṇi, ammā, ampā, aṭavi, āḷi, kaṭu, kāvēri, kucam, kūccam, kuṭi, kūṉ, kuḷam, kōṭṭi, kaṭṭil, cām, cā, paṭṭaṇam, poṉ, paḷḷi, veḷ mutaliyaṉa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya teṉcoṟkaḷām. iṉṉum ivi pōṉṟa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻivāyp paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻaracu talitāḻttapiṉ centamiḻārāycci cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka illāmiyāṉ, vaṭamoḻip pulavar tamiḻari oru peṭṭi miraṭṭu miraṭṭi vantaṉar. ippōtō moḻiyārāycciyāl eṇṇiṟanta vaṭamoḻi vāyccoṟkaḷ muṭavaṉ kutirikaḷāyt tirumpi varukiṉṟaṉa. cila  tamiḻar vaṭamoḻi vāyppaṭṭa coṟkaḷi yaṟintiruntum  avaṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṟat tuṇintilar.
meyyuṭi yoruvaṉ colamāṭ ṭāmiyāṟ
poypō lummē poypō lummē
vaṭanūlār tamiḻccoṟkaḷik kaṭaṉkoṇṭapiṉ avaṟṟit  tamiḻ vaṭivil vittiruppatilli. uṭaṉē urut teriyātu māṟṟi viṭuvar. maṟikkāṭu, paḻamali pōṉṟa iṭappeyarkaḷikkūṭa vētāraṇyam, viruttācalam eṉṟu moḻipeyarppārāyiṉ vēṟu eṉ colvatu? ivvāṟu ettaṉiyō teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟu vākukarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avi naḷarāṅ kālamum naṇṇum.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷatellām vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟu moḻi nūluṇarātār cilar koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. mūla mutaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻ tavira vēṟu enta moḻiyāvatu piṟacoṟka ḷillātatilli. oru colli iṉṉa moḻiyeṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakutip poruḷiyum ataṉiṉṟum tirinta palvēṟu coṟkaḷiyum pārkkavēṇṭum. avi enta moḻiyiluḷḷaṉavō anta moḻikkē atu uriyatām. viṉic collāyiṉ puṭipeyarcci (Conjugation) vēṇṭum. peyarc collāyiṉ  bgaÇy¡fz«(Declension) vēṇṭum. kal eṉṉum viṉi  kaṟṟāṉ, kaṟṟāḷ, kaṟṟār, kaṟṟatu, kaṟṟaṉa, kaṟṟa, kaṟka, kaṟpittāṉ, kaṟkappaṭṭatu, kaṟṟilaṉ, kali eṉap pala vaṭivukaḷilum puṭipeyara vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyilō atu kalā  (fiy) eṉat toḻilākupeyarāy maṭṭum niṟkum. iḵtōr utāraṇam.
cila vaṭamoḻip paṟṟuṭiyār muṉivar eṉṉum collukku muṉi eṉpatu pakutiyeṉ ṟaṟiyāmal, mavuṉam ceypavar muṉivar eṉṟu vaṭamoḻivaḻi yurippar. aiyaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ muṟippeyari āriyaṉ eṉpatiṉ citivākak kūṟuvar. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉic coṟkaḷukkum avar vaṭamoḻiyik kaṟpippatuṇṭu.
oru vakuppārāvatu piṟa vakuppāruṭaṉ iyipiṉṟit tāmāy ulakattil vantavarallar. aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ tavira oru moḻiyāvatu piṟamoḻiyiṉ kalappiṉṟit tāṉāy vantataṉṟu eṉṟu teḷika.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' kaṭakam 1931
2
vaṭamoḻit teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām oru perun tāymoḻiyi ṉiṉṟum utittavi yeṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūl (History), kulanūl   (Ethnology), moḻinūl (Philology) mutaliya pala nūlkaḷālum  valiyuṟuttappaṭum. āyiṉum, ticiccol vakiyil oru vakuppār putiyaṉavāy ākkik koṇṭaṉavum,tāymoḻiyiṉiṉṟum putiyaṉavāyt tirittukkoṇṭaṉavumāṉa iruvakic coṟkaḷ ovvoru moḻikkum ciṟappākum. iv viruvakic coṟkaḷum  oru kiḷimoḻikkuriya ataṉ tāymoḻiyic cārum; oru tāymoḻik kuriyavi oru perun tāy moḻiyic cārum.   tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi nōkkat tamiḻ oru tāymoḻi yum, āriya moḻikaḷi nōkkat tamiḻ oru perun tāymoḻiyumākum. eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭamiyālum, āriyac coṟkaḷil pala urutteriyātavāṟu attuṇit tirintuḷḷatiṉālum,  pala vaṭacoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ciṟappuc coṟkaḷākavē tōṉṟāniṟkum. ēṉic coṟkaḷi itupōtuḷḷa teṉcoṟ ṟuṇikoṇṭē teṉcol leṉak kāṭṭal moḻinūlāṉ muṭṭiṉṟi muṭivatākum. iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷāyiṉ  iv varampiṟ kuṭpaṭā.
oru colli oru moḻik kuriyateṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakuti (stem) allatu mūlam (root) emmoḻiyiluḷḷa teṉṟu aṟital  vēṇṭum.
oru colliṉ pakuti allatu mūlam palamoḻiyi liruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil, pakuti iyaṟkiyāṉateṉṟum, poruḷ ciṟantateṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
pala moḻiyilum, pakuti vaṭivilum poruḷilum ottiruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil ataṉ tiricoṟkaḷuḷa veṉṟum, aviyum pala moḻiyiliruppiṉ, etil avi kaḻipalaveṉṟum kāṇṭal vēṇṭum.
ettuṇiyō teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉṟu paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñaruṅkūṭap pakarntu varukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu : 
akaṅkāram.  akam + kāram. akam - avvulakam, vāṉulaku, 
mōkṣam. akaraccuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟantatu; cēymiyiluḷḷa vāṉulakattik kuṟippatu; ampar eṉṉum coṟpōla. a - ampar - amparam. ikarac cuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟanta ikam, impar eṉṉum coṟkaḷ aṇmic cuṭṭāy ivvulakattik kuṟittal kāṇka.
viṇṇulakiyum maṇṇulakiyum cēymi aṇmipaṟṟi avvulakam ivvulakam eṉpatu marapu.
``aruḷillārk kavvulaka milli poruḷillārk
kivvulaka millāki yāṅku''	(kuṟaḷ. 247)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum. 
vīṭu eṉṉum muttip peyar illatti uṇarttiṉāṟpōla, akam eṉṉum peyarum illatti uṇarttum. vīṭu = viṭutali; viṭuvatu vīṭu. piṟavit tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutali ceyvatu pēriṉpa vīṭu. veyiṉ maḻit tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutali ceyvatu ivvulaka vīṭu.
il eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyar ēḻām vēṟṟumi urupāy uṭpākattik kuṟittāṟpōla, akam eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyarum uṭpākattiyum uḷḷattiyum kuṟikkum akameṉ kiḷavi eṉat taṉittamiḻ ilakkaṇamākiya tolkāppiyattuḷḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kāram:  kari eṉṉum viṉiyaṭiyāp piṟanta toḻiṟpeyar. kari  + am = kāram;  mutaṉili tirintu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
cf: paṭi + am = pāṭam. tavi + am = tāvam.

3
vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkun
tolkāppiyat teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa makkaḷellām orutāy vayiṟṟiṉareṉṟu carittira ārāycciyāl teḷivākiṉṟatu. tamiḻar, teluṅkar, karunaṭar,  maliyāḷar, tuḷuvar mutaliya tirāviṭa vakuppiṉarellārum eṅṅaṉam  oru kālattil oru vakuppiṉarā yiruntaṉarō,   aṅṅaṉamē turēṉiyar, cēmiyar, āriyar eṉṉum mupperuṅ kiḷiyiṉarum oru kālattil orē vakuppiṉarā yiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ vakupput tiriyat tiriya avarkaḷ  moḻiyun tirintukoṇṭē vantirukkiṉṟatu. paṭippuk kālantoṭṭu oru vakuppār piṟa vakuppāruṭaṉ kalavātu pirintiruntāloḻiya avarkaḷ moḻi taṉimoḻiyā yiruttal eṭṭuṇiyum kūṭātatākum. peṟṟōr moḻikaḷiyē piḷḷikaḷum pēcukiṉṟaṉar. uṟaviṉarellārum oru moḻiyē pēcutal iyalpu. ākavē, iyipuṭiya vakuppiṉa rellām iyipuṭiya moḻikaḷiyē pēcal vēṇṭum. camaṟkiruta āriyar mūvaki moḻikaḷum kalakkiṉṟa mēliyāciyāviṉ vaṭapuṟattil ātiyiṟ kuṭiyiruntaṉar. aṅkiruntu avarkaḷ vaṭintiyāviṟku varumuṉṉarē avarkaḷ moḻiyil piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉa kalantuviṭṭaṉa. āriya varukippōtu intu tēya muḻutum vaḻaṅki vantavi tiruntiṉavum tiruntātaṉavumāṉa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷē. āriyarkaḷ vaṭa intiyāvi lirukkumpōtu pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷum teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyapiṉ vēṟu pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷum camaṟkirutattiṟ kalantuviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻkkalvi talitāḻttuc carittira aṟivum moḻinūlārāycciyum illāta iṭikkālattu, camaṟkirutamē intiya moḻikaṭ kellām tāy eṉṉumōr paṭiṟṟuri tamiḻp pakiñarāl tamiḻnāṭṭilum parappappaṭṭatu. camaṟkiruta akarātiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām camaṟkirutamē yeṉṉuṅ kuruṭṭu nampikki tamiḻaruḷḷattilum kuṭikoṇṭatu. mēṉāṭṭuk kaliccuṭar mēmpaṭṭut tikaḻum ikkālattuṅkūṭap pala tūya  teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar pēraṟiñarāyiṉārum. tamiḻ, ulakat tāymoḻiyiṉ iyaṟki vaṭivum camaṟkirutam ataṉ ceyaṟki vaṭivumātaliṉ,  av viru moḻikkum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷuḷ, tamiḻil mūlapporuḷ taruvaṉa vellām tamiḻeṉṟē perumpāluṅ koḷḷaṟpālaṉa. camaṟkirutam eṉṉum peyarē naṉṟāyc ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutaliṉ camaṟkirutattiṉ ceyaṟkiyi yuṇarttum.
tamiḻiṟ piṟkālattu vaṭacoṟkaḷ perituṅ kalantiruppiṉum paṇṭit tamiḻnūlkaḷ attuṇik kalappiṉavalla. kālattiṟ piṉṉōkkic cellac cellat tamiḻ nūlkaḷil vaṭacol vaḻakku arukik koṇṭē cellum. iṟṟi muḻuttamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ muṉṉiyatu tolkāppiyamātaliṉ atil vantuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷ āci, viciyar pōṉṟa aintāṟē yākum. vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkum ataṉ piṟa coṟka ḷellām tūya teṉcollē yātal veḷḷiṭimali. āyiṉum ārāycci yillārkku aṟivippāṉ vēṇṭi avaṟṟi muṟiyē ovvoṉṟā yeṭuttu moḻinūṟpaṭi viḷakkuvām.

poik eṉṉum collē vaṭamoḻiyil pratimā eṉṟākum. vaṭamoḻiyil prati eṉṟa coṟku mūlamilli. mā vṉpatugṇpuppeyarÉkutiyaṉṟu.puâkh, aṇimā, makimā mutaliya māvīṟṟup peyarkaḷi yellām pakāppataṅkaḷeṉṟē koḷḷuvar vaṭanūlār.

moḻimutaṉ meyyoṭu rakaram cērtta vaḷavāṉē tūya teṉ coṟkaḷiyum vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar tamiḻaṟiñar. iṉi vēṟupala māṟutal kūṭiṉ avar mayaṅkutalic collavum  vēṇṭumō?

4
`ilakkiyam', `ilakkaṇam'
vaṭamoḻiyum teṉmoḻiyum eṉatu uṉatu eṉṟu, eṅkun toṭarn tetirvaḻakkāṭaṟ kiṭamāṉa eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷuḷ, `ilakkiyam', `ilakkaṇam' eṉpaṉa iraṇṭām.
ulakil aṭuttaṭuttu vāḻum makkaḷ tammuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum poruḷkaḷi māṟṟik koḷvatu pōlum, aṭuttaṭuttu vāḻum vakuppār allatu nāṭṭār tammuṭ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷi māṟṟik koḷvatu pōlum, aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷum tammuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum coṟkaḷi māṟṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, ulakamoḻikaḷellām orutāy vayiṟṟiṉaveṉṟu moḻi nūl vallār kaṇṭa muṭiviṟkēṟpa, aṭuttu vaḻaṅkātu neṭṭiṭiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaṭkum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ potuvāyiruttali ārāycciyāṟ kāṇalām. āṅkilam ēṟattāḻa 6,000 milkaṭkappāl vaḻaṅkiṉum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟiṉ viriviyellām eṉatu moḻinūluṭ (Evolution of Language: Vol.1. The Primitive Language and the PreAryan Tamils) kaṇṭukoḷka. utāraṇamākac cila coṟkaḷi īṇṭuk kāṭṭutum.

iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa iṉṟiyamiyāta coṟkaḷi akarāti muṟiyiṉum, kalimuṟiyiṉum, eṉatu moḻinūluḷ eṭuttuk kūṟiyirukkiṉṟām. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
ic coṟkaḷ ellām āṅkilattiṉiṉṟum tamiḻukku vantavi yalla. āṅkilam varumuṉṉarē ivi tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. āṟāyiram kalliṭiyiṭṭa āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkum ittuṇip potuc coṟkaḷirukkumāyiṉ, ēṟattāḻa 5,000 āṇṭukaḷāka aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkum vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum ettuṇip potuc coṟkaḷiruttal vēṇṭum? ip potuccoṟkaḷellām moḻi nūṉ muṟiyil kūrntu nōkkuvārkkut tūya teṉcoṟkaḷeṉṟē tōṉṟā niṟkum, vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum potuvāyuḷḷa coṟkaḷi, vaṭacolleṉpārum teṉ colleṉpārumām irucārār moḻi nūlaṟiñar. vaṭamoḻiyip pōṉṟē teṉmoḻiyum ciṟantatoru tāymoḻiyātaliṉ, vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum teṉmoḻikku vantuḷḷa coṟkaḷip pōlavē teṉmoḻiyiṉiṉṟum vaṭamoḻikkuc ceṉṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷum paṉṉūṟṟukkaṇakkiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu `ilakkiyam', `ilakkaṉam' eṉpaṉa.
1. āriyar vaṭintiyāviṟku varumuṉṉarē (B.C. 3000) tamiḻil ilakkaṇamiruntamikku, 
``kaḷaviṉuṅ kaṟpiṉuṅ kalakka millāt
talivaṉun taliviyum pirinta kālik
.....     .......             .......         .........
uḷḷuṟut tiṟiṉē yuyarkaḻi yāṉantap
piyu ḷeṉṟu paḻittaṉar pulavar''	(yāpparuṅkalavirutti mēṟkōḷ)
eṉṟu akattiyam vaḻinūṉ muṟimiyil āciriya vacaṉaṅ kūṟuvatē cāṉṟām. mēlum, eḻuttilakkaṇam, collilaṇakkam, yāppilakkaṇa meṉṟa mūṉṟumē ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkiya miyampalākat tamiḻ pōlumoru ceyyuḷ moḻikku mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ taṉ nuṇṇaṟivāl vakuttiruttal kūṭum. aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi ēṉimoḻikaṭ kellāmillāta poruḷilakkaṇamum, icit tamiḻ nāṭakat tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, āciriyarākat tamiḻarāka oruvarē oruṅkēyiyaṟṟiṉar eṉpatu eṅṅaṉ poruntum? akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇamaṉṟē? ātalāṉ muttami ḻilakkaṇam oruvarāṉaṉṟip palarāṉ muṟiyē neṭṭiṭi yiṭṭiṭṭē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
akattiyar potiyañ ceṉṟatum, murukaṉpāṟ cuvaṭi peṟṟatumāṉa purāṇak katikaḷum tamiḻnūliṉ toṉmutu paḻimiyi uṇarttuvaṉa vākum.
akattiyarum tolkāppiyarum mutaṉūlāciriyarik kuṟikku miṭattu, yāṇṭum palarpālākavē kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. āriyar varaviṟku muṉ tamiḻilakkaṇa miruntiruppiṉ ataṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷum tamiḻiliruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. tamiḻil iyal eṉṉum col ilakkaṇattik kuṟippiṉum, atu atiyē variyaṟuttuc cuṭṭāmiyāṉum, kuṇam, iyaṟki eṉap palaporuḷ paṭutalāṉum, iyaṟcol, iyaṟpeyar, iyaṉmoḻi, iyaṟpaṭa moḻital mutaliya kuṟiyīṭukaḷil ilakkaṇattilum vēṟāka vevvēṟu poruḷ tarutalāṉum, muttamiḻil ovvoṉṟum ilakkaṇam ilakkiya meṉa iruvakippaṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟik kuṟikka vēṟu colliṉmi yālum, iyal eṉpatu muttamiḻil oṉṟaṉ ilakkaṇamu milakkiyamumāta lāṉum `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tamiḻukkiṉṟi yamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉavām.
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntami ḻēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila vilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''
eṉṟu tamiḻp pulavar tarukkumāṟu `attuṇi nirampiya vilakkaṇatti yuṭiya tamiḻmoḻiyil, ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷik kuṟikkac collilliyeṉiṉ atu eṭṭuṇiyum nampaṟkiṭaṉākātu.
vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇattik kuṟikka viyākaraṇameṉṟōr taṉic colluḷatu. teṉmoḻiyil ilakkaṇattit tavira vēṟoṉṟumilatu. ēṉi moḻikaṭkellā millāta aintilakkaṇam nirampiya taṉit tāy moḻikku ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷic cuṭṭac collillā tirukkumō?
3. `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷum, `lakṣaṇam, `lakṣyam' eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷum muṟiyē vaṭivilum poruḷilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.
	tamiḻ	camakirutam
ilakkaṇam =	1. Grammar 	lakṣaṇam =	1. Grammar
	2. aḻaku		2. aḻaku
	3. Definition		3. Definition
	4. kuṇam		4. kuṇam
	5. oḻuṅku		5. oḻuṅku
ilakkiyam =	1. Literature	lakṣyam =	1. Literature
	2. tiruṣṭāntam		2. tiruṣṭāntam
	3. poruḷ		3. poruḷ 
lakṣyampaṇṇavilli - poruṭpaṭuttavilli.
ākavē, ic colliṇikaḷ oṉṟiṉiṉṟoṉṟē tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum. eccolliṉiṉṟum eccolleṉpatē kūṭā. itukāṟum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷilellām, `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṉumiraṇṭum lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripeṉṟē kūṟap paṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. nūlāciriyarum uriyāciriyarumāṉa piṟṟi yilakkaṇi kaḷuṭ perumpālār vaṭanūl vaḻiyarātalāṉum, akkālattu moḻi yārāycciyiṉmiyāṉum, avar vaṭanūlvaḻi kūṟiyatellām maṟuppiṉṟi vaḻivaḻi vaḻuvappaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum ēṉi moḻikaḷellām ērāḷamākavum tārāḷamākavum kaṭaṉ koḷḷu meṉṟum, aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl vaḻakkiṟkum moḻipeyarppiṟkum pōtiya coṉṉirampāveṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyoṉṟē piṟamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu oru collum kaṭaṉ koḷḷātu taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭiyateṉṟum oru tavaṟāṉa koḷki, iṉṟum cila vaṭamoḻiyāḷark kiruntu varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ vaṭamoḻittuṇi ciṟitumiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭiya teṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar kūṟiyiruppavum, ataṟku māṟāka vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkal tamiḻkkuc cālāteṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyāḷaraṉṟic tamiḻppulavar cilaruṅkūṭat talivirittāṭu kiṉṟaṉar. (vaṭamoḻivāṇar tammip pukaḻak karutiyō, allatu vaṭamoḻivāṇarākat tammik kāṭṭak karutiyō terikilam.) tamiḻar moḻinūlēyaṟiyā reṉṟum cuyamāṉa teṉcoṟkum vaṭacoṉ mūlaṅka kaṟpippatē avarkaḷukkup perumiyeṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār (Bishop Caldwell) kūṟiyiruppatu iṉṟum meyyāyiruppatu peritum iraṅkattakka toṉṟām. tamiḻāḷar cilar teṉcoṟka ḷiṉṉiṉṉaveṉṟu aiyantiripaṟa vuṇarntiruntum avaṟṟi veḷippaṭuttum uṟutiyiṉṟi, vaṭamoḻiyar vāymaruṭṭuk kañci, ``viṇpaṭṭa kokku vallūṟu kaṇṭaṉṉa'' vilavilakkiṉṟamiyiṉ avar kāṭci ūmiyaṉ kaṇṭa kaṉāppōla oruciṟitum payaṉpaṭātu oḻikiṉṟatu.
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam'  eṉṟa coṟkaṭkut teṉcol vaṭivamē iyaṟkiyākavum vaṭacol vaṭivam ceyaṟkiyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru col oru moḻiyiṉiṉṟum maṟṟōr moḻikkuc ceṉṟateṉpataṟku ataṉ pakutipporuḷ allatu mūlapporuḷē ātāram. utāraṇamāka venna eṉṟa teluṅkuc coṟku mūlap poruḷiṉmiyiṉ atu veḷḷi neyyeṉap poruḷpaṭum veṇṇey eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripeṉat tuṇiyappaṭum. emmoḻiyil atu peruvaḻakkō, emmoḻiyil ataṉ tiricoṟkaḷ (Derivatives) peruntokiyaṉavō, ammoḻikkē aḵtu uritteṉat tuṇiyappaṭum.
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭku vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pakutip poruḷilatu; teṉmoḻiyilō teḷivāyuḷatu. yātōveṉiṟ kūṟutum.
1. ilakku eṉṉuñ col mutaṉmutal iliyi uṇarttum. ōrilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu. īrilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku.
2.  iraṇṭāvatu kuṟiyi uṇarttum, paṇṭikkālattil, vilvīrar oru marattiluḷḷa ōriliyit tēṭi ilakkāka vittu eytu payiṉ ṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. pāñcāli maṇattiṉum ilakkaṇi maṇattiṉum, ilakkāka iṭappaṭṭa maccam ilivaṭivāyiruttal kāṇka. ikkālattum kalleṟivallār ōr marattiṉuḷḷa kāykaḷiyē kuṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. akkālattil vilvīrar ilikaḷik kuṟik koṇṭami itaṉālum anumikkappaṭum. iliyē kāyiṉum ciṟitāyum niliyāṉatāyu miruttal kāṇka.
3. mūṉṟāvatu kuṟikkappaṭṭa ōr iṭatti uṇarttum. atukāli lekku eṉṟu tiriyum, iraṇṭu, reṇṭu eṉṟāṟ pōla, L. locus, a place, local, locality, location  mutaliya coṟkaḷ itaṉiṉṟum piṟakkum. lekku eṉṉuñ col teṉṉāṭṭiṟ peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
4. nāṉkāvatu kuṟikkappaṭṭa utāraṇatti uṇarttum
5. aintāvatu cittiratti uṇarttum. makkaḷ eḻuttaṟiyumuṉṉarē ōviyam aṟintiruntaṉar. aḻaku aṉivar kaṇkaḷiyum kavartaliṉ, āṭi, cuvar mutaliyavaṟṟil, ili koṭi mutaliya pala vaṭivukaḷi yeḻutivantaṉar.
``māṭakkuc cittiram'' eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.  ettuṇiyō aṟivu vaḷarcci peṟṟa ikkālattum maranuṇviṉi (Wood Carving), ulōka nuṇviṉi (Engraving), ōviyam (Drawing), kaṇṇuḷviṉi (Painting)  mutaliya cittira vēlikaḷellām marañceṭi, koṭi, ili, pū, kāy, kaṉiyākavē yiruttal kāṇka. āṭikaḷiṉ karikaḷellām perumpālum tāvara vaṭiviṉavē. itaṉāṟ cittiram ilittoḻileṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. iliyeṉpatu ēṉi yuṟuppukaṭkum iṉavilakkaṇam (upalakṣaṇam). ``ilimukap pimpūṇ'' eṉṟār kumarakuruparar. ulakap poruḷkaḷil tāvaram pōl kaṇkavar kaviṉpeṟu kāṭciyaṉa vēṟeviyumilli. itaṉālēyē koṭi, koṭiyiṭi, kulakkoṭi, kompu, kōti, kulāmāli, malarmukam eṉa mātar pukaḻappaṭuvatum, tiruviḻākkaḷil tōraṇam pantar mutaliyavi tāvarap poruḷkaḷāl puṉiyappaṭuvatu meṉka.
ilaṅkum eṉṉum viṉi, cittiratti yuṇarttum ilakku eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟum tirintatē.
ilaṅkutal viḷaṅkutal, ilakkam viḷakkam.
6. āṟāvatu cittiraveḻutti (Hieroglyph or Picture Writing) yuṇarttum. itupōtuḷḷa variyeḻuttēṟpaṭumuṉ ellā nāṭukaḷilum cittira veḻuttiḷ mūlamāyē makkaḷ tattam karutti veḷiyiṭṭu vantaṉar. oru cittiram oru vākkiyattiyāvatu oru ceytiyiyāvatu kuṟippā luṇarttuvatāyiruntatu.
7. ēḻāvatu variyeḻutti yuṇarttum. cittiraveḻuttut tōṉṟic ciṉṉūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉ variyeḻuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. av variyeḻuttum kūrntu nōkkiṉ tāvara vaṭivē tāṅki niṟkum. cappāṉiyam, cīṉam mutaliya moḻiyeḻuttukaḷ ikkālattum cittira vaṭivē peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
eḻututal eṉṉum viṉi paṭam varitaliyum eḻutteḻutu taliyum potuvāyuṇarttum.
eḻuttu ilakku (ili) vaṭivāyiruttaliṉ eḻututal ilakkitta leṉappaṭṭatu.
``ivvuruvu neñceṉṉuṅ kiḻiyiṉmē viruntilakkit 
tavvuruvu niṉippeṉṉun tukilikiyāl varuttittu''	(cīvaka. 180)
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil ilakkittu eṉpatu eḻuti eṉṉum poruḷil vantuḷḷatu. iv viṉiyiṉiṉṟē lēkakaṉ, lēkiṉi, likitam mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
ilakku, ilakki eṉpaṉa eḻutu eṉap poruḷpaṭum viṉip pakutikaḷ allatu ēval viṉikaḷām.
ilakku eṉpatu mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarākavumirukkum; atu ākupeyarāy eḻutti yuṇarttum.
Gk. logos = a word
	  a discourse
	  a treatise
	  a science
e.g: Theology, Physiology
ilakkiyam eṉpatu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar. ilakku pakuti; iyam vikuti.
cf.	kaṇ + iyam = kaṇṇiyam.
	kaṇṇutal = karututal, matittal
ilakkiyam eṉpatu toḻilāku peyarāy eḻuttiyum, karuviyāku peyarāy nūlkaḷiyum uṇarttum, nūluṇarttuḻi irumaṭiyāku peyar.
cf. Literature from letter.
ilakkiyattil iyam eṉṉum vikutiyit taṉiccollākkic cāttirameṉap poruḷ kūṟal īṇṭup poruntātu.
iṉi, ilakkaṇam eṉṉum collum ōr vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyarēyām. ilakku pakuti. aṇam vikuti.
cf. kaṭṭu + aṇam = kaṭṭaṇam
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum mutaṉmutal, eḻutum toḻili yuṇarttip piṉpu ākupeyarāy eḻutti yuṇarttum. ilakkiyam eṉpatu eḻuttālāya nūṟṟokutiyi yuṇarttiṉāṟpōla, ilakkaṇameṉpatu eḻuttālāya moḻiyiṉ oḻuṅki yuṇarttum.
cf. Grammar from gramnos, (Gk.) a letters. 
iṉi, ilakkaṇameṉpatu maṟṟōr vikutit toḻiṟ peyar. ilakku pakuti; am vikuti. eṇ ilakka vaṭivāy atāvatu eḻuttu vaṭivā yiruttaliṉ ilakkameṉappaṭṭatu.

iṉi eṇ eṉṉuñ col tarukkatti yuṇarttuvatu pōla, ataṉ pariyāyap peyarākiya ilakkameṉpatum tarkkatti yuṇarttum.
Logic from Logos (Gk)
``ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar eḻutteṉpariṉ pulavōr'' eṉṉumaṭiyil, eṇ tarukkattiyum eḻuttu ilakkiyattiyum (Literature) uṇarttutal kāṇka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷum ilakku eṉṉum pakutiyaṭit toḻiṟpeyarkaḷeṉṟum, avi ākupeyar muṟiyāṉ muṟiyē nūṟṟokutiyiyum moḻi yoḻuṅkiyu muṇarttumeṉṟum, ilakku eṉṉuñ coṟku mutaṟporuḷ iliyum vaḻipporuḷ eḻuttu mutaliyaṉavumeṉṟum, iviyellān tamiḻēyeṉṟum, āriyat tamiḻk kalappiṉ piṉ tamiḻiṉiṉṟum āriyañ ceṉṟa eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷuḷ ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉa iraṇṭeṉṟum, ivi lakṣyam lakṣaṇameṉa āṇṭut tiriyumeṉṟum, iṉṉum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum veḷḷiṭimali pōṟ ṟeḷḷitiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
kutiri tiruṭiṉāṉ oruvaṉ, nītipati muṉ kutiri mukatti mūṭi itaṟku ekkaṇ kuruṭeṉṟa uṭiyāṟku, iṭakkaṇ kuruṭeṉṟu akappaṭṭāṟ pōla, eṇṇiṟanta teṉcoṟkaḷik kavarntukoṇṭa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, ic coṟku mūlameṉṉi yeṉṟōr tamiḻaṉ kaṭāva, itaṟku mūlamilli, iḵtō riṭukuṟiyeṉ ṟakappaṭṭuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
iṭukuṟippeyarē tamiḻkkilli.
``ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē''
``moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā''
eṉpavi tolkāppiyam.
iṭukuṟip peyar vaṭamoḻiyil ērāḷam. pakutiyaṟiyappaṭāta teṉcoṟkaḷiyellām kaṇṇi mūṭikkoṇṭu iṭukuṟiyeṉap pakarntuviṭuvar.
kutiri tiruṭiṉāṉ kutiriyiṭam ceṉṟapōtu, atu uṭi yāṉik kaṇṭāṟpōṉ makiḻāmipōl, vaṭanūlār cila teṉcoṟkup pakutipporuḷ kaṟpikkiṉum atu ciṟitum poruntā tākiṉṟatu.
ākavē, moḻinūlārāycci vaṭacollum teṉcollum pakuttuṇartaṟkuc ciṟantatoru karuviyām. itaṉiyaṟintu kaṭippiṭikka.
- ``tamiḻppoḻil'' viṭi 1934
5
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam'
emmoḻic coṟkaḷ?
tamiḻiṉ tāymiyi vaṭavar neṭuṅkālamāy maṟittuḷḷami kāraṇamāka, ovvoru tamiḻccolliyum tamiḻccol leṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟku arumpāṭu paṭavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. aṅṅaṉam arumpāṭupaṭ ṭāyntu takka cāṉṟum ētuvuṅ koṇṭu nāṭṭiyum, ati nampāta perumi tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkē uḷḷatu.
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṟa iru collum teṉcollē yeṉṟu pallāṇṭiṟku muṉṉarē poḻilil takka cāṉṟukāṭṭi niṟuviyirukkiṉṟēṉ. āyiṉum, aṟivāṟṟaliṉmiyālō, aḻukkāṟ ṟoṭukūṭiya cerukkiṉālō, palar ati uṇarntatākat teriyavilli. ātalāl, ataṉi mīṇṭum mika viḷakkamākat terivikkat tuṇintēṉ.
cilar, ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarākavum tamiḻaṉparākavu miruntum, tām vaṭamoḻi payiṉṟuḷḷami kāraṇamākat takiyaṟat tarukki, yāṉ kaṇṭa moḻinūṉ muṭivukaḷi nampātatōṭu tākkiyum varukiṉṟaṉar. moḻiyaṟivu vēṟu, moḻinūlaṟivu vēṟu eṉpati iṉimēlāyiṉum avar uṇar vārāka.
`ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṟa iru coṟkaḷum teṉcollā eṉṟu ārāyumuṉ, avaṟṟāl kuṟikkappeṟum iraṇṭum teṉmoḻiyi luṇṭā eṉṟu kāṇalvēṇṭum. ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamum toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻukku iruntuvaruvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, mutaṉ mutalākavum avi nāvalantēyattil eḻunta moḻi teṉmoḻiyē eṉpatu mikiyaṉṟu. ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamum toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻukku iruntiruppiṉ, avaṟṟik kuṟikkac collum tamiḻil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟku `ilakkaṇam', `ilakkiyam' eṉṟa irucol tavira vēṟu teṉcolluṇṭā veṉiṉ, etuvu milli.
cilar, ilakkiyattiṟkuc `ceyyuḷ' eṉpatum, ilakkaṇattiṟku `nūl' eṉpatum tamiḻppeyar eṉpar. ceyyuḷ eṉpatu yāppu vaṭiviṟkup peyar eṉpatit tolkāppiyar yāppilakkaṇaviyalukkuc ceyyuḷiyal eṉṟu peyariṭṭatiyum, av viyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvē māttiri mutaliya      26 uṟuppukaḷiyum, ammi mutaliya eṭṭoṭu kūṭṭi,
``nallicip pulavar ceyyuḷ uṟuppeṉa
vallitiṟ kūṟi vakutturit taṉarē''
eṉṟu kūṟuvatiyum, 127ām, 128ām nūṟpākkaḷ ceviyuṟiyum aṅkatamum paṟṟivarum ceyyuḷkaḷi muṟiyē ceviyuṟic ceyyuḷ eṉṟum, aṅkatac ceyyuḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvatiyum,
``eḻunilat teḻunta ceyyuḷ teriyiṉ'' 	(162)
``ceyyuḷ moḻiyāl cīrpuṉintu yāppiṉ'' 	(234)
``ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa nāṭi'' 	(241)
eṉṟa piṟaviṭattellām `ceyyuḷ' eṉṉum col yāppu vaṭivattiyē kuṟippatiyum,
``pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
yāppiṉ vaḻiyatu eṉmaṉār pulavar'' 	(78)
eṉa yāppu eṉṟu muṉṉark kūṟiyatiyē,
``eḻunilat teḻunta ceyyuḷ teriyiṉ''
eṉac ceyyuḷ eṉṟu piṉṉar āciriyar peyar māṟṟik kūṟiyatiyum, nūlukkuri ceyyuḷ urinaṭi ākiya iru vaṭivilum paṇṭik kālat tiruntatiṉāl avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟāṉa ceyyuḷ vaṭivuriyē eḻunilattuḷ oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa teṉpatiyum, ceyyuḷ urinaṭi ākiya iraṇṭiyum taḻuviyatē ilakkiyam eṉpatiyum nōkkik kāṇpārāka. kāviyam (Epic) tamiḻil toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ eṉappaṭṭatiyum kāṇka.
ceyyuḷik kuṟittaṟku yāppu, pā, pāṭṭu mutaliya piṟa peyarkaḷu muṇṭēyeṉiṉ, aviyellām parupporuḷil oruporuṭ collāyiṉum nuṇporuḷil vēṟupaṭṭavi eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. av vēṟupāṭāvaṉa:
yāppu = metrical composition
ceyyuḷ = poem, poetry
pā = variety of metre.
pāṭṭu = ode
tūkku = metrical rhythm.
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu taṉippaṭṭa ceyyuḷiyum (Poem), ceyyuṭ ṭokutiyiyum (Poetry), ceyyuḷ naṭiyiyum kuṟikkum.
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu ceyyappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷiyuṭiyatu. cey, pakuti; uḷ, vikuti.
``vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ''
eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.
poem allatu poetry eṉṉum āṅkilaccollum itē poruḷik koṇṭiruppatu viyakkattakkatu.
E. poem, (from) F. poeme, (from) L. poema, (from).
Gk. poema - poiema - poieo = make.
Gk. poiesis - poesis = making, poetry, E. poesy.
Gk. poietes - poetes = maker, poet, E. poet.
iṉi, nūl eṉṉuñ colli ārāyvām. nūl eṉpatu, oru moḻiyiṉ allatu kaliyiṉ allatu vēṟētēṉumoru poruḷiṉ oḻuṅki allatu iyalpi eṭuttuk kūṟum puttakattiyum, ataṉ piṉpu ākupeyarp poruḷil atupaṟṟiya kaliyiyuṅ kuṟikkumē yaṉṟi, av voḻuṅki allatu iyalpiyē kuṟikkātu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, oru moḻiyiṉ oḻuṅkippaṟṟi viṉaviṉāl, ataṉ ilakkaṇameṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭpatu muṟiyākumēyaṉṟi, ataṉ nūl eṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭpatu muṟiyākātu. atu vaḻakku maṉṟu. ilakkaṇam eṉpatu grammar allatu definition eṉṟum, nūl eṉpatu treatise eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu ilakkaṇa nūliyum, piṉṉatu oru nūlāl allatu nūṟṟokutiyāl uṇarttappeṟum aṟivuk kaliyiyum (Science) kuṟikkumpōtu ākupeyarām. logos eṉṉum kirēkkaccol, mutalāvatu colliyē kuṟittup piṉṉar āṅkilattil uriyāṭṭiyum nūliyum kaliyiyum kuṟittami kāṇka. āyiṉ, ip piṟporuḷkaḷil atu logue eṉṟum logg eṉṟum tirivatuṭaṉ, tokiccoṟkaḷiṉ īṟākavum (suffix) māṟiviṭum. ilakkaṇam eṉṉum col nūlik kuṟikkumpōtu moḻiyilakkaṇa muṭpaṭṭa ellāk kaliyilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum kūṟum puttakaṅkaṭkellām potuppeyar eṉṟum vēṟupāṭu uṇartal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, orucilar pulam, iyal eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil ilakkaṇattik kuṟikkum eṉpar.
pulam eṉṉum colliṉ iyaṟporuḷ aṟivu eṉpatē. iccolliṉ aṭip piṟanta pulami pulavar eṉṟa coṟkaḷum, aṟivuṭimi aṟiñar eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. ittakiya potuccol moḻiyilakkaṇattic ciṟappākac cuṭṭutaṟkup poruntātu.
``pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval''
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār kūṟiyiruppiṉum, atu piṟarāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷap paṭṭatum peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkiyatum illi. aṟivunūlkaḷellā vaṟṟiyum potuvākap pulam eṉpatu ceyyuḷ vaḻakkam. ``pulampuri yantaṇar'' eṉṉum paripāṭaṟṟoṭaril (6 : 45) maṟinūl pulam eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ōr ākupeyar. vētam eṉṉum vaṭacollukkum aṟivu eṉpatē iyaṟporuḷākum. [É¤(=m¿.) - ntj«]. paṉampāraṉār kūṟṟil uḷḷa pulam eṉṉuñ col, potuvāka aṟivu allatu aṟivunūl ellāvaṟṟiyum kuṟippatāyiṉum, iṭam nōkkic ciṟappāka ilakkaṇatti allatu ilakkaṇa nūlik kuṟippatākak koḷḷappaṭum. āṉāl, itu āṭcipaṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi iyalpupaṟṟiyataṉṟu. itaṉāl pulam eṉpatu ilakkaṇap peyarākātu.
iṉi, iyal eṉṟa collōveṉiṉ, atu ellāp poruḷukkum uriya taṉmiyi allatu iyalpip potuppaṭiyākac cuṭṭuvataṉṟi vēṟoṉṟum kuṟiyāmiyiṉ, ilakkaṇap peyarātaṟku eḷḷaḷavum ēṟṟataṉṟām. iyal = taṉmi. iyaṉmoḻi vāḻttu = oruvaṉuṭiya ciṟanta taṉmikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟip pukaḻvatu. iyal = iyalpu, iyaṟki.
piṟa moḻikaḷil ilakkaṇam tōṉṟu muṉpum, āriyar varumuṉpum toṉṟutoṭṭum, tamiḻil ilakkaṇa, ilakkiyam iruntuvaruvatuṭaṉ, iṉṟum, tamiḻiltāṉ, piṟa moḻikaḷukkellā millāta poruḷilakkaṇamum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. itaṉālēyē, 
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''
eṉṟu parinturittār parañcōti muṉivar,
iṉi, ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭamoḻi vaṭivāṉa lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉpavaṟṟiṟku vaṭamoḻiyil, grammar, literature eṉṉum poruḷuṇṭā veṉiṉ, atuvumilli. vaṭamoḻiyil avaṟṟiṉ poruḷāvaṉa:
lakṣaṇa = kuṟi, aṭiyāḷam, iyalpu, taṉmi, kuṇam, aḻaku, (mutaliyaṉa).
lakṣyam = kuṟikkappaṭṭatu, variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu, varaṇikkap paṭṭatu, kuṟikkōḷ, nōkku, kuṟikkōḷuṭiyatu (mutaliyaṉa).
iv viru coṟkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭik karuttum kuṟi eṉpatēyākum. āṉāl, ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam, uŠ¢(ranj) eṉpataṉ tirivāṉa rakta (rakta) eṉṟum, lak (lag) eṉṟum, iruvēṟu vakiyākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
rañc = varaṇam pūcappeṟu, niṟaṅkoḷ, viḷaṅku, oḷir.
ic col raṅ (rang) eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
lak = ici, iṇi, kaṭṭu, oṭṭu.
ic col, lego eṉṉum kirēkkac collukkum, ligne eṉṉum ilattīṉ collukkum iṉac collākum.
lakṣaṇa, lakṣya eṉṉum coṟkaḷuṭaṉ, lakṣam (nūṟāyiram), lakṣmaṉ allatu lakṣmaṇaṉ, lakṣmī mutaliya coṟkaḷum, vaṭamoḻiyil toṭarpuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. lakṣmaṉ, lakṣmaṇaṉ=ciṟanta kuṟikaḷi allatu nallilakkaṇaṅkaḷi yuṭiyavaṉ. lakṣmī = naṟkuṟiyi allatu atirṣṭatti yuṭiyavaḷ;  atirṣṭacāli allatu teyvam.
lakṣmī eṉṉum collukkuc cilar lak (lag) eṉpatiyum, cilar lañc (lanj) eṉpatiyum, mūlamākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. lañc = oḷir, viḷaṅku, tikaḻ.
lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷum, vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭātatōṭu, ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷippōla, kuṟipporuḷiyē aṭippaṭiyākavum, viḷaṅkutaṟ poruḷ tarum ilaṅku eṉṉum colliyē mūlamākavum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. itu mikak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇattik kuṟikka vyākaraṇam eṉṉum collum, ilakkiyattik kuṟikkac cākityam eṉṉum collum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻilō ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷit tavira vēṟetuvumilli. aḵtōṭu, av viru coṟkaḷukkum vēr tamiḻiltāṉ uḷḷatu. aḵteṅṅaṉa meṉiṉ, kāṭṭuval:
eḻuccip poruḷi yuṇarttum ekara veḻutti aṭiyākak koṇṭa el eṉṉuñ col, eḻukiṉṟa allatu utikkiṉṟa katiravaṉi yum ataṉ oḷiyiyum kuṟikkum.
``ellē yilakkam'' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (iṭi 21)
el = katiravaṉ, veyil, oḷi, pakal.
el - eṉṟu = katiravaṉ.
eṉṟu - eṉṟavaṉ = katiravaṉ
eṉṟu - eṉṟūḻ = katiravaṉ mutircci, kōṭi.
ekaram allatu ēkāram eḻuccipporu ḷuṇarttuvati, eḻu, empu, evvu, ekku, eṭu, ēṇ, ēṇi, ēṇi, ēttu, (ēṟṟiccol), ēntu, ēl, ēṟu, erect, edify, eructate mutaliya coṟkaḷiṟ kāṇka. uyarccipporuḷum eḻuccip poruḷō ṭokkum.
el - elku - elaku - ilaku = viḷaṅku.
coṟkaḷiṉ mōṉittiripil ikaram ekaramākavum ekaram ikaramākavum tirivatu iyalpē.
ilaku - ilaṅku = viḷaṅku.
ilaṅku = ēri, kuḷam.
teḷinta nīr paḷiṅkupōl viḷaṅkutalāl, ēri ilaṅku eṉappaṭṭatu pōlum!
``mīṉka ṇaṟṟē ataṉcuṉi'' eṉṟu kapilar paṟampumalic cuṉiyi nilāviṟku oppiṭṭuk kūṟiyatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañci = ēri, kuḷam.
lañcu (lanj), raṅku (rang) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaṭku ilaṅku eṉṉum teṉcollē mūlamākum.
ilaṅku - laṅku - raṅku (va.)
laṅku - lañcu - rañcu - rakta (va.)
ilaṅku - ilaṅki = āṟṟiṭikkuṟi, tīvu, irāvaṇaṉ āṇṭa nakaram allatu tīvu.
perunīriṭippaṭṭa ciṟunilam taṉittu viḷaṅkuvatāl ilaṅki eṉappaṭṭatu pōlum!
ilaṅku-ilakku = viḷakku, viḷakkam.
ilakkutal = colippittal, viḷaṅkavittal, varital, eḻututal, kuṟittal. ``irīraki yilakkuka'' (civaca. potu. 274).
varaṇantīṭṭip paṭam varivatum eḻutteḻutuvatum viḷaṅkavitta lākum. mutaṟkālattil paṭaveḻuttē (Linoglyph) vaḻakkiliruntatu. iṉṟum, eḻututal eṉṉuñ col, varaṇappaṭam varitaliyum eḻut teḻututaliyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṇṇukku maṟintuḷḷa poruḷkaḷi viḷakku viḷakkutalpōla, eḻuttum kaṇṇukku maṟintuḷḷa karuttukaḷi viḷakkuvatākum. eḻututalum kuṟittalum oṉṟē.
ilakku - ilakki = eḻutu.
``ivvuruvu neñceṉṉuṅ kiḻiyiṉmē liruntilakkittu'' 	(cīvaka. 180)
ilakku allatu ilakki eṉṉun teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil lik eṉat tiriyum. itu mikamikak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
lik=eḻutu, likita=eḻutappaṭṭatu, lipi=eḻuttu, lēkak= eḻuttāḷaṉ, lēka=eḻutappaṭṭatu, lēkaṉī=eḻutukōl-ivvāṟu ilakku eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil palavāṟākat tiricoṟkaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇiyuḷḷatu.
ilakku=kuṟipporuḷ, ampeyyuṅ kuṟi, aṭiyāḷam, kuṟitta iṭam, kuṟitta camiyam, kuṟitta allatu nāṭiya poruḷ, porakkuṟitta etiri, kuṟitta aḷavu.
ilakku vittal = kuṟivittal, ilakkuppārttal = kuṟipārttal, camiyam pārttal - ivi iṉṟum tamiḻ vaḻakkām.
``ilakka muṭampiṭumpik keṉṟu kalakkattik
kiyāṟāk koḷḷātām mēl''		(kuṟaḷ. 627)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
oru kuṟitta iṭatti iṉṟum ilakku eṉpar nelli nāṭṭār. ilakku eṉṉum collē, ilattīṉil locus eṉṟu tirintu, āṅkilattil, local, locate, locative mutaliya coṟkaḷip piṟappikkum. kuṟitta iṭam eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷit tarum tamiḻccol, ilattīṉil taṉ ciṟappupporuḷi yiḻantu, iṭam eṉṉum potupporuḷiyē tarutal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē, oppōṉ kēṭṭalik kuṟikkum tā eṉṉum tamiḻccol, ilattīṉil kēṭṭali maṭṭum kuṟikkum do eṉṉum ticiccollākat tirintuḷḷatu.
eyvataṟkuk koṇṭa kuṟiyaṉṟi, eṭuttukkāṭṭuvataṟkuk koṇṭa kuṟiyum, ilakku eṉṟē collappaṭum.
``uṭaṉpiṟap paṉpiṟku ilakkāṉavar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ'' eṉṉum coṟṟoṭaril, ilakkāṉavar eṉpatu eṭuttukkāṭṭāṉavar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. ip poruḷil ilakku eṉṉum col ilakkiyam eṉṉum vaṭivam eytum. oru karutti allatu kūṟṟi ilaṅka (viḷaṅka) vikkum poruḷ ilakku eṉappaṭṭatu. āṅkilattiluḷḷa illustrate eṉṉum collum ip poruḷatē. L. illustro = to light up, to illuminate, il = in, lustro = to make light.
ilakku - ilakkam = oḷi, kuṟipporuḷ, eṇkuṟi, eṇṇiṭam, eṇ, pēreṇṇākiya nūṟāyiram.
ilakkamiṭutal = kaṇakkiṭutal.
mutalāvatu nūṟāyirattiṟkum nūṟu nūṟāyirattiṟkum tamiḻil taṉiccol illi. piṉṉar, pērilakkamākiya nūṟāyiram ilakka meṉṟum, kaṭici yilakkamākiya nūṟu nūṟāyiram kōṭi yeṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
ilakkam - lakṣa (va.) - yh¡ (c.)- lac (E.)
kōṭi - kōṭṭi (va.) - kuṭōṭu (u.) -- crore (E.)
ilakku - ilakkar, ilakkāram = āṭi.
āṭi āḷkaḷi viḷaṅkacceyvatāl ilakkar eṉappaṭṭatu.
``āṭi yillātavaṉ arimaṉitaṉ''
ilakku - ilakki = āṭi, campaḷam.
campaḷam oru kuṟiyetirppātalāl ilakki yeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañciṉi - ilāñciṉi = kuṟi, aṭiyāḷam, muttiri. ilañciṉi - ilacciṉi = kuṟi, muttiri. 
ilāñciṉi - lāñcaṉa (va.).
eḻuttik kuṟikkum ilakku eṉṉum col, ākupeyarāyc colliyum moḻiyiyum uṇartti, `aṇam' vikuti peṟṟu moḻiyiṉ nūṟṟokutiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
Logos eṉṉum kirēkkaccol colliyum, kaṭṭuriyiyum nūliyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. ac collum ilakkam eṉṉum teṉ collukku iṉamāṉatē.
Gk. gramma = a letter; gramma - grammar (E.)
L. litera = a letter; litera - literature (E.)
iṅṅaṉam, āṅkilattiluḷḷa ilakkaṇa vilakkiyap peyarkaḷum, eḻuttik kuṟikkuñ colliṉiṉṟē piṟantuḷḷami kavaṉikkattakkatu.
ilakkaṇam eṉṉum teṉcolliṉiṉṟē, lakṣaṇā eṉṉum vaṭacollum tirikkappaṭṭu, piṉpu tamiḻil ilakkaṇi eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
lakṣaṇā = kuṟippupporuḷ.
viṭṭa vilakkaṇi, viṭāta vilakkaṇi, viṭṭum viṭāta vilakkaṇi ākiya mūṉṟum kuṟippāyp poruḷuṇarttuvaṉavē. ilakkaṇik kiṉamāṉa ākupeyar kuṟippāyp poruḷuṇarttu vaṉavaṟṟōṭu (pavaṇantiyārāl) cērkkap peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tamiḻil, ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, vērp poruḷit tāṅki niṉṟu, eḻuttiyuṅ kuṟittu, Grammar, literature eṉṉum poruḷit tarukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyilō, avaṟṟukku nērāṉa lakṣaṇam lakṣyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vērccol aṟṟu, eḻuttik kuṟikkumpōtu lik eṉa vēṟoru vaṭivam pūṇṭu, ilakkaṇa vilakkiyattik kuṟikkātu kuṇaṅkuṟip poruḷiyē tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. iv vēṟupāṭṭik kaṇṭukoḷka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum col liraṇṭum teṉcollē yeṉṟu teṟṟeṉat teḷika. kulap piriviṉi yāl tamiḻaṉukku vanta tāḻvu, viṭutali yaṭinta piṉpum tamiḻit tākkātirukka.
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum iraṇṭiyuṅ kuṟikkat tamiḻil `aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam' eṉa vēṟiru coṟkaḷum uḷa. añcaṇaṅkam - aintilakkaṇam, añcaṇaṅkiyam - aintilakkiyam (yāḻppāṇa akarāti). aṇaṅkam - ilakkaṇam (potiki nikaṇṭu). aṇaṅkutal-olittal. ``pukiyaṇaṅka'' (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli 10, 18). ivi ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarātiyiṉ iṇippu maṭalattilum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aṇaṅku = oli, eḻuttu, aṇaṅku - aṇaṅkam = ilakkaṇam.
aṇaṅku - aṇaṅkiyam = ilakkiyam.
aṇaṅku eṉṉum viṉiccol tūya taṉittamiḻc collātalil, aṇaṅkam, aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum peyarkaḷi vaṭacolleṉat taliciṟanta tamiḻp pakivarum vāytiṟavār eṉpatu teḷitarutēṟṟam.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' ēppiral 1948
6
tiruveṉṉum col teṉcollē
maruva larkoḷa maṉporu ḷaṟṟapaṉ
marapi ṉāruḷar mintilar vāyiru
tiruvi ḻantapiṉ ṟiruveṉuñ colliyu
moruva niṉṟavaṉ tamiḻa ṉoruvaṉē!
mikamikac ciṟupāṉmiyarāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar paṉṉūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa teṉmoḻic coṟkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyil tirittukkoṇṭatu maṉṟi, collārāycciyum kalimuṟi yaṟivum mikka iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇ ṭilum avaṟṟi vaṭacoṟkaḷē yeṉṟu valippatum, vaṭacol vaṭivaṅ kaḷiyē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅka neñcaḻuttaṅ koḷvatum vintiyiṉum vintiyē!
tiruveṉṉuñ col āriyarāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar nāvalan tēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkivanta tamiḻccol leṉpataṟku, toṉmutu cārpu nūlāṉa tolkāppiyattilēyē atu āṭci peṟṟiruppatum mēli yāriyamoḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilēṉum atu ev vaṭiviṉum kāṇappaṭāmiyum, ataṉ vērpporuḷ vaṭamoḻi yilillātu tamiḻilēyē yiruppatum pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷām.
tiruveṉṉuñ col tamiḻil, 1. celvam, 2. tirumakaḷ, 3. ciṟappu,     4. aḻaku, 5. kānti, 6. polivu, 7. pēṟu(pākkiyam), 8. tūymi allatu teyvattaṉmi, 9 nalviṉi, 10, kaṇiyaṉ(cōtiṭaṅ  kūṟuvōṉ), 11. māṅkaliyam, 12. paḻiya taliyaṇi vaki, 13. makaḷir koṅkimēl tōṉṟum vīṟṟutteyvam, 14. tūymi allatu perumi kuṟikkum aṭimoḻi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷil toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu; aḻaku eṉṉum poruḷil tirumi eṉap paṇpup peyar vikuti peṟṟum, celvam perumi tūymi mutaliya poruḷkaḷil tiruvam eṉa ammīṟu peṟṟum, ak kuṇaṅkaḷik kuṟittut tiruvaṉ, tiruvāḷaṉ, tiruvāṭṭi, tiruvattavar, tiruviṉāḷ eṉa uyartiṇip peyarākiyum vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu. srī eṉṉum ataṉ vaṭacol vaṭivamō kaṭikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟkālattatāy, atuvum urinūlkaḷil, 1. celvam, 2. tirumakaḷ, 3. pēṟu, 4. aḻaku, 5. tūymi yaṭimoḻi eṉṉum aimporuḷkaḷilē vaṭamoḻiyāḷar toṭaṅkiviṭa, avarālum moḻi nūlaṟivum tāymoḻip  paṟṟum aṟṟa tamiḻarālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟatu. srī eṉpatiluḷḷa cakaram tamiḻc cakaratti yotta oliyiṉatā yiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tiruveṉṉuñ collukku mēṟkūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷuḷ  celvam, aḻaku, tūymi yeṉṉu mūṉṟē mukkiyamum piṟavaṟṟukku aṭippaṭiyumākum. am mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum, celvam eṉpatē mutalatākum.
celvattiṉāl oruvar ciṟakkavuṇṭu iṉpamāy vāḻvatāl   uṭal moḻumoḻuveṉa aḻaku peṟukiṉṟatu; vaṟumiyiṉāl vayiṟu kāyntu maṉaṅkavaṉṟu veyilil vāṭi mēṉi keṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉālēyē, celvamuḷḷa iṉattāri veḷḷokka leṉṟum vaṟumiyuṟṟa iṉat tārik kārokka leṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar nam muṉṉōr. celva mikutiyiṉāl oruvarkkup perumiyum teyvattaṉmiyum uṇṭākiṉṟatu; ulakiṟ celvamikka aracaṉ teyvat taṉmiyuṭiyavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaṭavuḷ ulakelāmuṭiya celvar. aḷaviṟanta matippē teyva vaṇakkamākum. matippuc celvattāl varuvatu. aṟivum oruvakic celvamē. teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa viṭamellām tūymi karutappaṭum.
āḻntu nōkkiṉ, tiruveṉṉuñ collukkuriya poruḷ kaḷellām mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum; nōkkik kāṇka. 
tiruveṉṉuñ col ippōtuḷḷapaṭi mutaṉmutalākat tol kāppiyattil, 
piṟappē kuṭimi āṇmi āṇṭō
ṭuruvu niṟutta kāma vāyil
niṟiyē aruḷē uṇarvoṭu tiruveṉa
muṟiyaṟak kiḷanta oppiṉatu vakiyē	(tol. meyp. 25)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvil, celvam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. piṉṉart tirukkuṟaḷil,
aḻukkā ṟeṉavoru pāvi tirucceṟṟut
tīyuḻi yuyttu viṭum,	(kuṟaḷ. 168)
aṟaṉaṟintu veḵkā vaṟivuṭiyārc cērun
tiṟaṉaṟin tāṅkē tiru,	(kuṟaḷ. 179)
ūruṇi nīrniṟin taṟṟē yulakavām
pēraṟi vāḷaṉ tiru 	,	(kuṟaḷ. 215)
iruvē ṟulakat tiyaṟki tiruvēṟu
teḷḷiya rātalum vēṟu,	(kuṟaḷ. 374)
nallārkaṭ paṭṭa vaṟumiyi ṉiṉṉātē
kallārkaṭ paṭṭa tiru,	(kuṟaḷ. 408)
viṉikkaṇ viṉiyuṭiyāṉ kēṇmivē ṟāka
niṉippāṉi nīṅkun tiru,	(kuṟaḷ. 519)
iṉattāṟṟi yeṇṇāta vēntaṉ ciṉattāṟṟic
cīṟiṟ ciṟukun tiru,	(kuṟaḷ. 568)
muyaṟci tiruviṉi yākku muyaṟṟiṉmi
yiṉmi pukutti viṭum,	(kuṟaḷ. 616)
irumaṉap peṇṭiruṅ kaḷḷuṅ kavaṟun
tirunīkkap paṭṭār toṭarpu,		(kuṟaḷ. 920)
karumaṇiyiṟ pāvāynī pōtāyām vīḻun
tirunutaṟ killi yiṭam	(kuṟaḷ. 1123)
eṉac celvam, (celvattiṟkut taliviyāṉa) tirumakaḷ, aḻaku  eṉṉum mupporuḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu; piṟa poruḷkaḷil piṟa nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu.
iṉi, iruvaki vaḻakkilum, tēviyal makaviyal eṉṉum iru vakic cārpil, tiru eṉṉum col tūymi, celvam, aḻaku, maṅkalam, kaṇṇiyam eṉṉum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟil aṭi moḻiyākat toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatip piṉvaruṅ  kāṭṭukaḷā laṟika.
1. tēviyal
(1) teyvam: tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ, tirumāl, tirumaṭanti.
nūl:	tirukkaṭikkāppu, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirukkuṟuntāṇṭakam,   tirukkōvi, tiruneṭuntāṇṭakam, tiruppatikam, tiruppallāṇṭu, tiruppāṭṭu, tiruppāvi, tiruppukaḻ, tirumantiram, tirumuṟi, tirumoḻi, tiruvakuppu, tiruvācakam, tiruvāymoḻi,  tiruveḻuttu.
ūṇ:	tirukkaṉṉalamutu, tiruppaṭimāṟṟu, tirumaturam, tirumāli vaṭi, tiruvamutu.
aṇi:	tiruccuṇṇam, tirunīṟu, tiruppaṭṭam, tiruppāvāṭi, tirumaṇ, tiruvātti.
poruḷ:	tirucciṉṉam, tirumaram, tirumalar, tirumuṭṭu, tirumuḷippāliki, tiruvalaku, tiruvāciki, tiruvāṭutaṇṭu, tiruviḷakku.
iṭam:	tiruccentūr, tirunakar, tirunāṭu, tirunelvēli,  tiruppati, tirumali. tiruvaraṅkam, tiruvitāṅkūr, tiruvēṅkaṭam, tirukkaṇ, tirukkaḷiṟṟuppaṭi, tirukkaṟṟaḷi, tiruccuṟṟu, tiruppāṟkaṭal, tirumaṭam, tirumāḷiki, tirumuṟṟam, tirumuṉ,  tiruvuṇṇāḻiki, tiruvōlakkam.
kālam:  tirunāḷ, tiruvātiri, tiruvōṇam.
ciṉi:	tirukkaṇ(cāttutal), tiruccevi, tirumuṭi, tirumēṉi,  tiruvaṭi, tiruvāy(malartal), tiruvuḷḷam.
kuṇam:	tirukkuṟippu, tiruvaruḷ, tirukkōlam.
toḻil:	tirukkaliyāṇam, tirukkārttiki, tiruviḻā, tiruvūṟal, tirukkāppu, tirukkūttu, tiruttoṇṭu, tiruppaṇi,  tiruppaḷḷi, tirumuḻukku, tiruviḷiyāṭal.
(2) aṭiyār:	tirukkūṭṭam, tiruccuṟṟu, tiruttakkatēvar,  tiruttoṇṭar, tirumūlar, tiruvaṭimār, tiruvaḷḷuvar, tiruvōṭu.
2. makaviyal
(1) vēttiyal: tirumaṟu, tirumukam, tirumuttu, tirumūppu, tirumantiram, tiruvāṇi, tiruvāykkēḷvi, tiruvōli.
(2) potuviyal: tirunilikkāl, tirunilimakaḷir, tiruppūṭṭu, tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ, tirumaṅkaliyam, tirumaṇam, tiruvāṅkutal, tiruvil, tiruviṉi, tiruvuḻuttu(aṇi).
ivaṟṟuḷ, tirunelvēli, tiruvaraṅkam mutaliya iṭappeyarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu ulakavaḻakkil tiruveṉṉum aṭipeṟṟē vaḻaṅku vatiyum, srīraṅkam eṉṉum vaṭivam iruvaki vaḻakkilum piṟkālatta tātaliyum nōkkuka. itaṉāl, tiruvilliputtūr eṉpatē srī villiputtūr eṉpataṉ paṇṭi vaṭivamum eṉa aṟika.
tirumakaṉ eṉṉum peyar tirumāṉ eṉa maruvic celvam allatu  perumi kuṟikkum aṭimoḻiyākum. perumakaṉ eṉṉum peyar perumāṉ eṉa maruvutal kāṇka. tiru eṉpati srī eṉṟu tirittataṉāl, tirumāṉ eṉṉum aṭiyi srīmāṉ eṉṟu tirippar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ataṉuḷ, māṉ eṉṉum īṟṟi mat eṉa maṟṟum tirittu, srīmat, srīmati eṉpavaṟṟi muṟiyē āṇpāl  peṇpā laṭimoḻikaḷāka muḻu vaṭavaṭivil vaḻaṅkuvar. tirumakaṉ, tirumāṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟkaṭkut tirumakaḷ, tirumāṭṭi eṉpavi tamiḻil muṟiyē peṇ pālākum. perumāṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟkup perumāṭṭi eṉpatu peṇpālātal kāṇka. srī eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭivit tamiḻil cī eṉac citittut tirumāṉ tirumāṭṭi eṉpavaṟṟic cīmāṉ cīmāṭṭiyeṉa eḻutuvatu, kōḻikkōṭu eṉpati Calicut eṉṉum āṅkilavaṭivu vāyilāyk kaḷḷikkōṭṭi yeṉṟeḻutuvatum, kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉpati Calcutta eṉṉum āṅkila vaṭivu vāyilāyk kalkattā eṉṟeḻutuvatum pōṉṟa aṟiyāmiyēyākum. itaṉāl, cīkāḷatti eṉṉum vaṭiviṉum tirukkāḷatti eṉṉum vaṭivē takkatākum eṉa aṟika.
iṉi, tiruveṉṉuñ colliyaṉṟic ciṟuveṉṉuñ colliyum srī eṉat tirittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. peṇṭirkkuc contamākavuḷḷa ciṟucelvattiṟku allatu celvattiṟkuc `ciṟupāṭu' eṉṟum ciṟutaṉam eṉṟum peyar.
ciṟutaṉan tēṭuvaḷ 		(taṇṭali. 95)
vīṭṭuc celavil mīttuvikkum ciṟu tokikkuc cilvāṉam eṉṟu peyar. ciṟutaṉam eṉpatu piṟkālattil taṉi vēṟṟu (private) niti eṉap potuppaṭavum poruḷ peṟṟatu.
uṭiyārsrī rājarāja tēvar ciṟutaṉattuk
koṭutta (S. I. I. ii, 3)
ciṟutaṉam eṉpatu srītaṉam, cītaṉam eṉat tavaṟāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
srī eṉpatit tiruveṉṟum, srīlasrī eṉpatit tirupperuntiru allatu tirumikutiru eṉṟum, makārājarājasrī eṉpati makavaraca varacattiru allatu māvaraca varacattiru eṉṟum, rājasrī eṉpati aracattiru eṉṟum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkal vēṇṭum. ma. (rā). (rā). srī eṉṉum mukavariyaṭiyil, srī eṉpatu tavira maṟṟa mūveḻuttukaḷum tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukaḷāyē yiruttalik kāṇka. itaṉāl, srī eṉpatu piṉṉi vaṭiva meṉpati yōrka.
cīrkāḻi eṉpataṉ mutal vaṭivam cīkāḻi eṉpar. cīr eṉṉuñ col centamiḻāyiruttaliṉ, cīrkāḻi yeṉṟē vaḻaṅkuvatu nallatu. itaṟ kiciyātār tirukkāḻi yeṉṟē vaḻaṅkutal  vēṇṭum.
iṉi, tiruveṉṉun tamiḻccoṟku vērpporuḷ eṉṉiyō veṉiṟ kūṟuvēṉ:
tiruveṉpatu til eṉṉum vēraṭiyāyp piṟantu tiraṭṭappaṭuvatu allatu īṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.

`kālam' eṉṉuñ col emmoḻikkuriyatu?
vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum potuvākavō, av viraṇṭa ṉuḷ oṉṟaṟkē ciṟappākavō uriya pala coṟkaḷpaṟṟi av viru moḻi yāḷarkkum toṉṟutoṭṭu nikaḻntuvarum pōr kaṟṟār aṉivarkkum terintatē. ac coṟkaḷuḷ  ‘fhy«’ eṉpatum oṉṟākum.
oru colli oru moḻikkuriyatu eṉṟu kūṟutaṟku oppiyaṉ bkhÊüš(Comparative Philology) aṟivu iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭap paṭum. intiyāvil, ciṟappāyt tamiḻnāṭṭil, moḻinūl eṉṉum peyariyē kēṭṭaṟiyātāruṅkūṭa, vaṭamoḻippaṟṟoṉṟē kāraṇamākap pala teṉcoṟkaḷi  vaṭacoṟkaḷākak kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. avaruḷḷum āṅkilak   kalvippaṭṭam peṟṟavarō veṉṟāl, tammi ellā moḻi kaḷilum vallunareṉṟē eṇṇikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. moḻinūl eṉpatu potuk kalviyiṉiṉṟum, allatu ilakkiya vilakkaṇak kalviyiṉiṉṟum vēṟāṉa oru taṉikkali eṉpati avar caṟṟum uṇarntilar.
potuvāka, vaṭamoḻip paṟṟiṉarellām vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷilum akarātikaḷilum uḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷē eṉṟu eṇṇikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. ulakattiluḷḷa palvēṟu moḻikaḷum tammuḷ evvaḷavō vēṟupāṭuṭiyavēṉum, oru marattiṉ palvēṟu kiḷikaḷ ōr aṭimarattiṉiṉṟē kiḷittuḷḷatu pōla, oru mūlamoḻiyiṉiṉṟē kiḷittuḷḷaṉavākum. cila coṟkaḷilum, ilakkaṇa neṟimuṟikaḷilum ulaka moḻikaḷellām ottiruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām. ulakattiluḷḷa palvēṟu makkaṭkulaṅkaḷum orutāy vayiṟṟiṉa eṉpatu iṟṟi moḻiyārāycciyāl teriyavarutaliṉ, avaṟṟāl pēcappaṭum palvēṟu moḻikaḷum oru mūlattiṉa eṉṟē koḷḷattakum.
aiyāyiram milukkappāṟpaṭṭa ilattīṉ, kirēkka moḻikaṭkum tamiḻukkum potuvāṉa cila coṟkaḷuḷa. 
e-ṭu: navis(L.) = nāvāy.  palios(Gk.) = paḻiya.
iru kulattār allatu nāṭṭār kalantu allatu aṭuttaṭuttu  vāḻiṉ, cila poruḷkaḷit tamakkuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum māṟṟikkoḷvatupōlac cila coṟkaḷiyuṅ māṟṟikkoḷvar. utāraṇamāka, āṅkilarukkum tamiḻarukkum toṭarpuṇṭāṉapiṉ Father(ghâÇ), Battalion(g£lhs«) mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ tamiḻilum, g©o(bandy), njh¥ò(tope)  mutaliya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ āṅkilattilumākak kalantuḷḷaṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyum teṉmoḻiyum aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkiṉamiyiṉ, ēṟkeṉavē iv viraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa potuc coṟkaḷ nīṅkalākap putitāka oṉṟiṉiṉṟoṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ ettuṇiyō palavām. vaṭamoḻiyilum tamiḻc coṟkaḷ allatu tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ uṇṭeṉpatik kālañceṉṟa kālṭuvel, kuṇṭarṭ eṉṉum iruperu moḻinūlārāycciyāḷar eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.   ivaruḷ muṉṉiyavar vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum teṉcoṟkaḷiyum  pirittaṟivataṟkum vitikaḷ vakuttirukkiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉavi iraṇṭu. aviyāvaṉa:
1. oru col enta moḻiyil palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉum iṉaccoṟkaḷuṭaṉum mūlapporuḷit tāṅkikkoṇṭu peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatō, anta moḻikkē aḵtu uriyatu eṉpatu.
2. vaṭamoḻiyilum teṉmoḻiyilum vaḻaṅkum oru col vaṭamoḻiyiṉ iṉamāṉa mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷ oṉṟiluṅ kāṇappaṭāmal, tamiḻiṉ iṉamāṉa maṟṟellāt tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiluṅ kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ atu tamiḻukkē uriya collām eṉpatu.
vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum, piṟa moḻikaḷellām taṉṉiṭattiṟ kaṭaṉkoḷḷat tāṉmaṭṭum oru collum piṟa moḻi yiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷātu taṉittiyaṅkum tūymi vāynta teṉṟum cila vaṭamoḻivāṇar poyyum puliyum malinta purāṇak kālattaṉṟi, ārāyccimikka ikkālattum pitaṟṟuvatu, mikavum eḷḷinakiyāṭat takkataṉṟi vēṟu eṉ ceyaṟpālatu?
vaṭamoḻi ikkālat tamiḻiṟ perituṅ kalantiruppatāl ēṟkeṉavē iv viraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa potuc coṟkaḷōṭu, putitāy vaṭamoḻikkuc ceṉṟa tamiḻ allatu tirāviṭac coṟkaḷiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷē yeṉṟu naṭunili ārāycciyillā vaṭamoḻip paṟṟiṉarellām nākkaṭippāka vāyppaṟi yaṟikiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilac coṟkaḷ nūṟṟukku muppatu vītam iṟṟit tamiḻil teruppēcciṟ kalantiruppiṉum, nūlvaḻakkiṟ kalantilatu. aṅṅaṉam orukāl nūlvaḻakkilum kalakkumāyiṉ, piṉṉar ārāycciyillātār all, beetle, crack, derive, ear, fear, galley.  bil, in, jury, kill, locality, mess, near, one, put, quoth, roll, shrink, through, un, vin. want, yean, zenith eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟkaḷē   muṟiyē ellām, viṭṭil, kiṟukku, tiri, ēr, veru, kalam, āli, il, cūḷ, kol, ilakku, mici, neruṅku, oṉṟu,  pōṭu, kūṟu, uruḷ, curuṅku, turuva, al, vīṇ, vēṇṭu, īṉ,  ceṉṉi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷākat tirintuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvaraṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamē vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ tiripākap pala tūya teṉcoṟkaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉareṉka.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvōr tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattiyum oruciṟitum nōkkukiṉṟilar. uṇmi eṉṉum kuṇam niṉivu, col, ceyal eṉṟa mūṉṟiyum taḻuviyatu eṉpati uṇarttaṟkut tamiḻil uṇmi
(uḷ = maṉam), vāymi(vāy), meymmi   (bkŒ = uṭampu) eṉa mūvēṟu coṟkaḷ uḷa. marattiṉ uṟuppukaḷil ovvoṉṟiṟkum ataṉataṉ vēṟupāṭṭiṟkēṟpa vevvēṟu peyar uḷatu. iliyeṉṉum orē yuṟuppiṟku ili, tāḷ, tōki, ōli eṉa nāṉku coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ovvoru collum ovvoru taṉmiyik kuṟikkum. pūviṉ vevvēṟu nilikaḷik kuṟittaṟku vevvēṟu coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mutalāvatu tōṉṟumpōtu arumpu; pērarumpāyiṉ pōtu; malarntapiṉ  malar; viḻuntapiṉ vī ; vāṭiyapiṉ cemmal. uḷḷattiṉ vevvēṟu toḻiṟkum vevvēṟu col uḷa. e-ṭu: uḷ(ḷu), to will; uṇar to feel, to comprehend; niṉi, to think, to remember; niṉivukūr, to remember, to commemorate; uṉ(ṉu), to imagine, (c‹d« = imagination); eṇ(ṇu), to deliberate, to consider; karutu, to conceive (fU¤J = concept, idea); aṟi, to know, koḷ, to opine, (nfhŸ = opinion, koḷki = doctrine); mati, to estimate, to regard; muṉ(ṉu), to propose; muṉṉiṭu, to propose, to set before, (K‹Å£L = having proposed, having set before, for the purpose of); tīrmāṉi, to determine, to resolve; naya, to appreciate; teruḷ, to perceive clearly. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻiṟ pala poruḷum paṟṟip pala coṟkaḷiruppavum ivaṟṟik kavaṉiyātum, ivaṟṟiṉ nuṭpavēṟupāṭukaḷi ārāyātum, ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkiyum, tamiḻiṟ poruḷuṇarttap pōtiya collilli yeṉṟu paḻittut tirivar.
ivaṟṟuḷ, mati yeṉṉuñ col ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa mūlamoḻic collākum. am mūlamoḻikkup piṟa moḻikaḷiṉum tamiḻ mika neruṅkiyateṉpatu moḻinūl ārāycciyāl viḷaṅkum. itupaṟṟik kālṭuvel aiyarum piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. (evarum eṅṅaṉamum aiyuṟāmip poruṭṭu avar kūṟṟiṉ mūlattiyē moḻipeyarkkātu)  iṅkut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
“Nevertheless, I regard the comparison of words, when carefully and cutiously conducted, as an important help to the determination of lingual affinities, and it will be found, I think, that the following vocabularies bear independent testimony, in their own degree, to the same result at which we arrived by grammatical comparison viz. that the Dravidian idioms exhibit traces of an ancient, deep-seated connection with pre-Sanskrit—the assumed archic mother-tongue of the Indo-European family, whilst at the same time the traces they exhibit of relationship to the languages  of the Scythian group, especially to the Ugrian tongues, are on the whole, closer, more distinctive, and more essential.”
- (Comparative Grammar of the Dravidian Languages, p. 452).
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē kālattik kuṟippataṟkum tamiḻiṟ pala coṟkaḷuḷa. avi kāl, poḻutu, vēḷi, koṉ, cevvi, aṟṟam, ēlvi, camiyam, nēram, ñāṉṟu, patam mutaliyaṉavām.
ivaṟṟuḷ kāl eṉpatu kāli, kālam eṉat tirintu neṭuṅ kālattiyum kuṟuṅkālattiyum kuṟikkum. ēṉiyavellām kuṟuṅ kālattiyē kuṟikkum. poḻutu eṉpatu pōḻtu, pōtu eṉat tiriyum. perumpoḻutu eṉpatu iru mātaṅkoṇṭa gUt¤ijí«(Season) ciṟupoḻutu eṉpatu nāṉmaṇi nēraṅkoṇṭa nāṭpakutiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum. vēḷi eṉpatu nāṭpakutiyik kuṟikkum; ceyyuḷil vēli eṉṟum tiriyum.  koṉ eṉpatu uriya tīya kālattiyum, cevvi eṉpatu takunta   naṟcamiyattiyum, aṟṟam eṉpatu takunta tīccamiyattiyum kuṟikkum. ēlvi eṉpatu elviyeṉṟuṅ kuṟukum. camiyam eṉpatu amiyameṉṟum tirintu, takunta vēḷiyi allatu paruvamāṉa niliyik kuṟikkum. nēram eṉpatu oru kuṟitta nērattiyum oru viṉi nikaḻccikkuriya fhy¤ijí«(duration) kuṟikkum. patam eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ cevviyāṉa niliyik kuṟikkum. ñāṉṟu eṉpatu pōtu eṉpatip pōṉṟatu.
kāl, kāli, poḻutu, vēḷi, ēlvi, camiyam, nēram, ñāṉṟu, patam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ. vantakāl, vantakāli, vanta poḻutu, vantavēḷi eṉa oru kuṟitta nērattiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
oru nāḷiṉ oḷi iruḷ vēḷikaḷik kuṟippataṟkup pakal iravu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum, oru nāḷiṟ kāli 6 maṇi mutal naṉṉāṉku maṇi nēramākavuḷḷa aṟuvakic ciṟupoḻutukaḷiyuṅ kuṟippataṟkuk kāli, naṇpakal, eṟpāṭu, māli, yāmam, vikaṟi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. vēlik kālattik kaṇippataṟku 24 nimiṭa nēraṅkoṇṭa nāḻiki eṉṉum aḷavuḷḷatu.
oru vārattiṉ ēḻu  nāḷkaḷum ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy,  aṟivaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, kāri eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ aṟivaṉ, kāri eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkum patilākap putaṉ, caṉi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. vāram, kiḻami eṉpaṉa urimi eṉṉum oruporuṭ   coṟkaḷ. iruvāram cērntatu  oru pakkam eṉappaṭum. itiyē pakṣam eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyār. (āṭit tiṅkaṭ pēriruṭ pakkattu cilap. 23 : 133. pakkam = pakuti; iruṭpakkam = kiruṣṇa pakṣam.)
irupakkam cērntatu oru tiṅkaḷ (mātam).
iru tiṅkaḷ cērntatu oru perumpoḻutu. cittiri mutal paṅkuṉi yīṟāka, ivviru mātamāy, muṟiyē, iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi eṉa āṟu perumpoḻutukaḷākum. ivi mikap paḻiya ilakkaṇamākiya tolkāppiyattiṟ collappaṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkē poruntuvaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa. ākiyāl, ivaṟṟukku  vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kūṟum peyarkaḷellām moḻipeyarppē.
paṉṉiru mātaṅkaṭkum ippōtu vaḻaṅkivarum peyarkaḷ vaṭa coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟukkum patilākap paṉṉīr irācippeyarkaḷē maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṟpōl paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭilum vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. paṉṉīr irācip peyarkaḷil mēṭam(mēḻam),  iṭapam, kaṭakam, kaṉṉi, tulām, kumpam, mīṉam eṉṉum ēḻum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ allatu tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻik kum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ mutalāṟukkum muṟiyē takar, kuṇṭi, alavaṉ, maṭanti, tūkku, kuṭam eṉṟum peyarkaḷuṇṭu. ēṉiya mituṉam, ciṅkam, viruccikam, taṉucu, makaram eṉṉum aintum tamiḻil muṟiyē iraṭṭi, āḷi, tēḷ, vil, cuṟā eṉappaṭum. irācikkut tamiḻil ōri eṉṟu peyar.
paṉṉiru mātaṅkaḷum irācip peyar peṟumāṟu:
 1. 	cittiri	-	mēṭam	 7.	aippaci	-	tulām	 
 2. 	vikāci	-	iṭapam	 8. 	kārttiki	-	viruccikam
 3. 	āṉi	-	mituṉam	 9. 	mārkaḻi	-	taṉucu
 4. 	āṭi	-	kaṭakam	10. 	ti	-	makaram
 5. 	āvaṇi	-	ciṅkam	11. 	māci	-	kumpam
 6. 	puraṭṭāci	-	kaṉṉi	12. 	paṅkuṉi	-	mīṉam
acuvaṉi mutal rēvati yīṟāṉa irupattēḻu naṭcattiraṅkaṭkum tamiḻiṟ peyaruṇṭu. avi muṟiyē puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟali, mūtiri, kaḻi, kāṟkuḷam, kaṭcevi, koṭunukam, kaṇi, uttaram, ki, aṟuvi, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉi, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, uṭikuḷam, kaṭikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākki, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟi nikaṇṭukaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
āṇṭu, muyaṟci, col, nañcu, makiḻcci, nōy, viruppam, ūtiyam, iḻappu, varuttam, nilā, āciriyaṉ, kōyil, nīr, muḻukku, kuḷippu, cōṟu, tēr, tiruviḻā, paṟavi, vilaṅku, mēlāṭi, illampukal, cāppāṭṭuk kaṭi, valamvaral, cilampu kaḻittal allatu, kāppuk kaḻaṟṟal, nīṭuvāḻi, mūtta makaṉ, tammaracu, irumiyum ottu eṉṉum centamiḻc coṟ kaḷukkum coṟṟoṭarkaḷukkum muṟiyē, varuṣam, pirayāci, vārtti, viṣam, cantōṣam, viyāti, iṣṭam, lāpam, naṣṭam, kaṣṭam, cantiraṉ, upāttiyāyar, ālayam, jalam, apiṣēkam, snāṉam, cātam, ratam, uṟcavam, pakṣi, mirukam, aṅkavastiram, kirukappiravēcam, pōjaṉacāli, piratakṣiṇam (ceytal), kaṅkaṇa visarjaṉam, cirañcīvi, jēṣṭa kumāraṉ, cuyarājyam, upayāṉu cammatamāy eṉṉum vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum paṉṉūṟṟuk kaṇakkiṉa. ivi pōṉṟaviyē paṉṉiru mātaṅkaṭkum irupattēḻu naṭcattiraṅkaṭkum itupōtu  vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷum eṉka.
 naṭcattiram tamiḻil nāḷ allatu nāṇmīṉ eṉappaṭum. potuvāṉatāyiṉ mīṉ eṉṟum uṭu eṉṟum veḷḷi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. oru pakalum ōr iravuñ cērntu oru kiḻami. oru kiḻamikkuriya naṭcattiram nāḷ.nāḷum kiḻamiyum nalintōrk killi eṉṟār auviyār. āyiṉum, nāḷ eṉpatu kiḻami yeṉṉum poruḷilēyē iruvaki vaḻakkilum perumpālum vaḻaṅkivarum. kirakam kōḷ allatu kōṇmīṉ eṉappaṭum.
aṟupatāṇṭiṟ korumuṟiyāka nikaḻnta cila pañcaṅkaḷpaṟṟi, allatu makkaḷiṉ carācari vāḻnāḷpaṟṟi, aṟupatāṇṭu eṉṉum aḷavu paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭil cila mutiyōrkkuḷ peyaraḷavāy maṭṭum vaḻaṅki vantatākat terikiṉṟatu. atu naṭimuṟiyiṟ kaṇikkappaṭavilli. cālivākaṉaṉālēyē aṟupatāṇṭiṟkum peyariṭappaṭṭatākat teri kiṉṟatu. avi yāvum vaṭacoṟkaḷ. avaṟṟukkut teṉcol illi. aṟupatāṇṭuc cakkaram cuṟṟic cuṟṟi varutaliṉ, atu āṅkila āṇṭu  pōlac  carittirattiṟkum neṭuṅkālak kuṟippiṟkum payaṉpaṭuvataṉṟu. paṇṭit tamiḻar avvav varacariṉ āṭcik kālattikkoṇṭē ōr āṇṭiyō neṭuṅkālattiyō kuṟittum kaṇakkiṭṭum vantatākak kalveṭṭiṉiṉṟum teriyavarukiṉṟatu. (e-ṭu: kōpparakēcari māṟa varmaṉukku 24ām āṇṭu). oru neṭuṅkālattik kaṇakkiṭuvataṟkup pala aracarkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālattik kūṭṭavēṇṭiyatirukkum. ēṉeṉil aṭikkaṭi aracar māṟuvatu vaḻakkam. (carittirakālattil) ōr  aracaṉ evvaḷavu nīṭittāḷiṉum nūṟāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭuvatu mikavum arumi. ik kuṟiyi nīkkutaṟku oru periyār kālattiṉiṉṟu, allatu oru nakarat tōṟṟattiṉiṉṟu, allatu oru peru nikaḻcciyiṉiṉṟu neṭuṅkālattik kaṇakkiṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟukku muṟiyē meykaṇṭāṉ yāṇṭum, kollam āṇṭum, kaliyāṇṭum utāraṇamākum. āṅkila mukammatiya āṇṭukaḷum periyār kālattiṉiṉṟum  kaṇakkiṭap paṭuvaṉavē.
aṟupatāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ūḻi eṉṉum aḷavu, paṇṭit tamiḻ nāṭṭil nikaḻnta cila kaṭalkōḷkaḷpaṟṟi, oru variyaṟiyiṉṟi īraḻiviṟ kiṭippaṭṭa allatu oru viri vaḷarcciyi (Evolution)  kuṟikkiṉṟa oru neṭuṅkālattik kuṟikka allatu kaṇikka vaḻaṅkivantatu. itu iv vāciriyar(tolkāppiyar) ātiyūḻiyiṉ  antattē in nūl (tol kāppiyam) ceytaliṉ eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tolkāppiyak kaṟpiyal  4ām cūttira vuriyiṟ kūṟutalāṉum, aimpūtaṅkaḷil ovvoṉṟum ovvōr  ūḻiyil tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟu paripāṭal (2 : 3-19) kūṟutalāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. ākiyāl itupōtu pañcāṅkattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa yuka aḷavu tamiḻar ūḻikkup poruntuvataṉṟu;  tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṭalkōḷ kaḷiṉ iṭikkālaṅkaḷ avvak kālattiṟ kaṇikkappaṭṭirukkalām. āṉāl, atippaṟṟiya oru kuṟippum namakkuk kiṭittilatu. iṟṟik kaliyūḻi taliccaṅkat tiṟutiyil nikaḻnta kaṭal kōḷiṉiṉṟum kaṇikkappaṭṭatāyiṉ, kaliyukam toṭaṅki 5041 āṇṭukaḷ āyiṉa eṉṉum kaṇakkup poruntuvatākum.
 mēṟkūṟiya kālap peyarkaḷuḷ kālam eṉṉuñ colliyē ciṟappākak kūṟa eḻunta tik kaṭṭuriyātaliṉ, ataṉ mūlattiyum atu emmoḻiyic cērntateṉpatiyum iṉi ārāyvōm.
kālam eṉṉum col, kāl eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟum piṟantatu. itu kāl eṉuñ col taṉittum, orukāl, vantakkāl eṉat toṭar moḻikaḷilum vantu kālapporuḷ taruvatāl   aṟiyappaṭum. tūṇ eṉpatu am īṟu peṟṟut tūṇam eṉṟu āṉāṟpōlak kāl eṉpatum am īṟu peṟṟuk kālam eṉṟāyatu. ai īṟupeṟiṉ kāli eṉṟākum; pār (kampi) eṉpatu pāri eṉṟāṉāṟpōla.
kāl, kāli, kālam eṉṉum mūṉṟu coṟkaḷum tamiḻil iruvaki vaḻakkilum mikap peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa.
kāl eṉpatu orukāl mukkālum eṉṉum toṭarkaḷilum, vantakkāl pōṉakkāl eṉa etirkāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷilum ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iv viṉiyeccaṅkaḷi vantākkā pōṉākkā eṉṟu citittu vaḻaṅkuvar tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭamēṟpakutiyi luḷḷavar.
piṉmuṉ kālkaṭi vaḻiiṭat teṉṉum
aṉṉa marapiṟ kālaṅ kaṇṇiya
eṉṉa kiḷaviyum avaṟṟiyal piṉavē	(tol. col. 714)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattil, kāl eṉpatu oru viṉiyecca vikutiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyam evvitattum iṟṟikku 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatākum.
kāli eṉpatu oru nāḷiṉ ciṟanta kāli allatu vēḷiyākiya viṭiyaṟkālatti uṇarttum. kāli, viṭikāli eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku. kāliyum māliyum kikūppik kāltoḻutāl eṉac ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum ac col ap poruḷil vantatu. kāli eṉṉum col muṟpakaliyum, muṟpakaliṉ   muṟpakutiyiyum kuṟippatumuṇṭu.
iruvīṟṟum urittē cuṭṭuṅ kāli
iciyuṭa ṉarukum teriyuṅ kāli
eṉat tolkāppiyattil, kāli eṉṉuñ col poḻutu, vēḷi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷpōla oru kuṟitta nēratti uṇarttiṟṟu.
kālam eṉṉum col oru kuṟitta kālattiyum viṭiyaṟ  kālattiyum paruva kālattiyum oru kālap pakutiyiyum potuvāṉa kālattiyum uṇarttum. viṭiyaṟkālam, cāyaṅkālam irākkālam, pēṟukālam, tuṉpakālam, kālattiṉāṟ ceyta naṉṟi(kuṟaḷ. 102), āyiyal niliyuṅ kālat tāṉum(tolkāppiyam) eṉṉun toṭar kaḷil oru kuṟitta kālamum kālattālē yeḻuntu (kāttāli yeḻuntu eṉpatu kocci vaḻakku.) eṉṉun toṭaril viṭiyaṟkālamum; vēṉiṟ kālam(kōṭikālam), maḻikālam(kārkālam) mutaliya toṭarkaḷil paruvakālamum; muṟkālam, taṟkālam, kaṟkālam, kīḻōrk kākiya kālam(tol.) mutaliya toṭarkaḷil oru kālappakutiyum poruḷiṭam kālam ciṉikuṇam toḻil eṉṉun toṭaril potuvāṉa kālamum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āṅkilattil `Tense' eṉṟu kūṟum viṉiyilakkaṇattik kuṟikkat tamiḻil kālam eṉṉum collē koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum	(tol. col. 683)
kālan tāmē mūṉṟeṉa moḻipa	(tol. col. 684)
iṟappiṉ nikaḻviṉ etirviṉ eṉṟā
ammuk kālamum kuṟippoṭuṅ koḷḷum	(tol. col. 685)
viṉiccol kālaṅkāṭṭuvatāl kālakkiḷavi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. kiḷavi-col.
mārik kiḷaviyum pallōr paṭarkkik
kālak kiḷaviyoṭu muṭiyum eṉpa 	(tol. col. viṉi. 10)
tamiḻc ceyyuḷuṟuppukaḷ irupattunāṉkaṉuḷ kālamum oṉṟākat tolkāppiyattuṭ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kēṭpōr kaḷaṉē kālavakieṉāa...	 (tol. poruḷ. cey. 1)
aḵṟiṇi muṭipupeṟum uyartiṇic coṟkaḷuḷ kālam eṉpatum oṉṟākak kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar.
kālam ulakam uyirē uṭampē

 pālpirin ticiyā uyartiṇi mēṉa	(tol. col. kiḷavi. 58)
 kālam eṉpatu vaṭacollāyiṉ, tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟappukunta tolkāppiyattil ataṉik kūṟār āciriyar.
kāl eṉṉuñ coṟkut tamiḻiṟ pala poruḷkaḷiruppiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ nāṉkē talimiyāṉavum, piṟavaṟṟiṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉavu mākum. avi fhš(leg), kālvāy, kāṟṟu  (kāl + tu), kālam eṉpaṉa vākum, in nāṉkaṉuḷḷum, kūrntu nōkkiṉ, mutalatē ēṉi mūṉṟiṟkum mūlamākum.
oru coṟkup pala poruḷiruppiṉ avi yāvum orē toṭar puṭiyaṉavā yirukkum. toṭarpiṉṟēl orē vaṭivuḷḷa vevvēṟu coṟkuriyaṉavā yirukkum. iṅkuk kāl eṉṉuñ coṟkuk kūṟiya nāṉku poruḷum orē toṭarpuṭiyaṉavē, eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, nīṇṭatu eṉṉum karuttiyē avi nāṉkum taḻuviyuḷḷaṉa. kāl eṉṉum uṟuppu (oṭuṅki) nīṇṭiruppatu; kālvāy nīṇṭu ōṭuvatu; kāṟṟu ōriṭattu nillātu tici neṭuka nīṇṭu vīcuvatu; kālam oru muṭiviṉṟi nīṇṭu celvatu.  in nīṭcik karuttuk kāl eṉṉum uṟuppiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiya tākum.
kāl eṉṉum uṟuppiṉ peyar kāl eṉṉum viṉiyiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyatākum. kālutal cintutal, allatu poḻital. mēkaṅkaḷ maḻi poḻivatu kālutal eṉappaṭum. tūrattil maḻi poḻiyumpōtu, tūṇkaḷ pōlap pala paṭalaṅkaḷākap pirintu vīḻvati eṉṟum pārkkalām. av vīḻccikaḷ maḻikkālkaḷ eṉappaṭum. ak kālkaḷ mēkattiṟku allatu vāṉattiṟkut tāṅkalpōlak kīḻē nīṇṭu nilattil viḻuvatupōl, uṟuppākiya kālum ōr uṭampiṟkut tāṅkalāyk kīḻē nīṇṭu nilattiṟ pativatāl, uvamiyāku peyarāyk kāleṉappaṭṭatu. piṉṉart tāṅkuvatu, aṭiyil iruppatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟik kāl eṉpatu pala poruḷ kaṭkup peyarāyiṟṟu. muḻaṅkāl  uṭampiṟ kāṟpakutiyātaliṉ kāleṉpatu nāliloru paṅkiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. itaṟku maṟutali yākak kūṟuvar ilakkaṇikaḷ.
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarātiyil (Lexicon) kāl eṉṉum  coṟku 44  poruḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aviyāvaṉa:-
 1. 	kāṟpaṅku	17. 	makaṉ
 2. 	`va' eṉṉum ilakkam	18. 	vamicam
 3. 	pātam	19. 	iṉamuṟi
 4. 	pūviṉ tāḷ	20. 	piṟappiṭam
 5. 	aṭippākam	21. 	vāykkāl
 6.	  eṉṟa eḻuttu	22. 	pirivu
 7. 	uruḷ	23. 	vaḻi	
 8. 	vaṇṭi	24. 	naṭi
 9. 	kōl	25. 	iṭam
10. 	kuṟuntaṟi	26. 	vaṉam
11. 	necavuttaṟiyiṉ miti	27. 	viṉmuṉi
12. 	kippiṭi	28.	marakkāl
13. 	tūṇ	29. 	aḷavu
14. 	paṟṟukkōṭu	30. 	piṉṉutaṟku vakutta mayirppakuti
15. 	muḷi	31. 	kiraṇam 
16. 	marakkaṉṟu	32. 	maḻikkāl
kāl =	1. kāṟṟu
	       2. vātanōy
      	       3. pañcapūtam
kāl =	1 poḻutu	4. kālaṉ
	            2. cevvi	5. iyamaṉ
	            3. taṭavi	6. karuniṟam
kāl =	1. 7ām vēṟṟumi urupu
	           2. viṉiyecca vikuti
	            3. ōr upacarkkam.
in nāṟpattu nāṉku poruḷkaḷum, kālpōlak kīḻiruppatu, 
tāṅkuvatu, nīṇṭiruppatu, pirintiruppatu, kāṟpaṅkāyiruppatu, nilattil ūṉṟuvatu mutaliya karuttuppaṟṟiyaṉavāyē iruttal  kāṇka.
kāl uṭampil ōr iṭamāyiruttalāl, ataṉ peyar iṭattiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. pantarkkālukkup perumpālum mūṅkili naṭuvatālum, mūṅkil vaḷarvatu kāṭṭilātalālum,  mūṅkiṟkuḻāyi aṟuttu mukattaṟ karuviyākak koḷvarātalālum, kāl eṉṉum peyar mūṅkil, vaṉam, marakkāl eṉṉum poruḷkaḷiyum tarum.
poḻutu mutaliya mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷum kālattiyum, kālaṉ mutaliya mūṉṟu peyarkaḷum maḻikkāliyum mūlamākak koṇṭaṉa vākum.
āṅkilattil ‘column’ eṉṉum col tūṇiyum piriviyum caṭṭattiyum kuṟikkum. caṭṭamum kāl, tūṇ, kālvāy ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟip pōṉṟē irukkum. kāl eṉṉum col kālattik kuṟikkumpōtu tamiḻilum am īṟu peṟum. tūṇ eṉpatu tūṇam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ākiyāl kāl ‘column’ eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭku ōr iyipuṇṭō eṉṟu aiyuṟakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. āṅkila akarātikaḷ ‘column’ eṉṉum coṟku celsus(high), collis(a hill) mutaliya mūlaṅkaḷik   kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avi avvaḷavu poruttamuṭiyaṉavalla. moḻi nūṟkali iṉṉum cariyāy vaḷarkkappaṭāmiyiṉ, mēṉāṭṭār tamiḻiṉ arumiyi aṟintilar eṉṟē kūṟalvēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, cilar, tamiḻukku nīṇṭa kālattik kuṟikkac collilli yeṉṟum, putaṉ, caṉi, rutu eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkut teṉ collilli yeṉṟum, 27 naṭcattiraṅkaḷukkum, 12 mātaṅkaṭkum tamiḻiṟ peyarilli yeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkā teṉṟum tamiḻarkkut taṉi nākarikamilli yeṉṟum, palavāṟu pitaṟṟāniṟpar. iṉi, cilar muḻuppūcaṉiyic cōṟṟil maṟippatupōl, tamiḻē vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantatutāṉ eṉṟu varampiṟantu maṉappāl  kuṭippar. tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku mika muntiya teṉpatum, vaṭamoḻit tōṟṟattiṟku allatu āriyar varukikku muṉṉarē, akkālattu makkaṭkut tōṉṟiya karuttukaḷi yellām terivikkum coṟkaḷit taṉpāṟ koṇṭuḷḷa teṉpatum avar aṟiyār pōlum!
āriyar in nāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉarē tamiḻar pala kalikaḷilum tērntiruntatiṉālum, tamiḻarkku vāṉanūlum (Astronomy) terin tiruntami paṇṭi nūlkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvatālum, noṭi mutal ūḻiyīṟāṉa ciṟiya periya kāla aḷavukaṭkellām tamiḻiṟ colluṇmi yālum, colvaḷam tamiḻiṉ taṉip paṇpātalālum, kāl eṉpatu oru viṉiyecca vikutiyākavum, kālam eṉpatu viṉiyiṉ kālattik kuṟikkuñ collākavum toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻilakkaṇattil vaḻaṅkivaruvatālum, kāl, kāli, kālam eṉṉum mūṉṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷum kuṟuṅkālattiyum neṭuṅkālattiyum kuṟikkat tamiḻil iruvaki vaḻakkilum peruvaḻakkākat toṉṟutoṭṭē vaḻaṅkivaruvatālum, kālam eṉṉum colliṉ mūlapporuḷ  tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟuvatālum, mēṉāṭṭāriya moḻikaḷil kālam eṉṉum col kālapporuḷil vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyapaṭi kālam eṉṉuñ col centamiḻc collē yeṉṟu eṭṭuṇiyum aiyamaṟat teḷiyappaṭum.
tamiḻiṟ cila karuttukaḷi yuṇarttaṟku itupōtu collilli  yeṉpatu uṇmiyē. āyiṉum ataṟkut takunta kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa.
1. kiṟittuvukku muṟpaṭṭa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻnūlkaḷuḷ, tolkāppiyam tavira maṟṟaviyellām aḻintu pōyiṉamiyiṉ, avaṟṟiluḷḷa aruñcoṟkaḷum maṟintupōyiṉa.
2. paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyik kaṭal koṇṭami yiṉ, ap pakutiyil vaḻaṅkiṉa eṇṇiṟanta ticiccoṟkaḷ aṟavē maṟintaṉa.
3. tamiḻar tam aṟiyāmipaṟṟi arumiyāṉa teṉcoṟkaṭkup  patilāka, vaṭacoṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉatiṉālum,paṇṭik kālattil virivāṉa tamiḻakarātikaḷ eḻutappaṭāmiyālum, vaḻakkaṟṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷellām maṟintoḻintaṉa.
4. tamiḻ, maṉita camūkattiṉ iḷamiyil tōṉṟiya mikap paḻimi yāṉa moḻiyātaliṉ, ikkālattiṟkuriya cila karuttukaḷ akkālat tamiḻarkkut tōṉṟavilli. ataṉāl avaṟṟukkuc collun tōṉṟavilli.
5. ceṉṟa 2000 āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻ āriyavayappaṭṭuḷḷami yiṉ, putuccoṟkaḷ puṉintu taṉittamiḻ vaḷarkkappaṭavilli.
ākiyāl, tamiḻip piṟa puṉmoḻikaḷpōṟ karuti iḻittuk kūṟuvatu takātu.
oru poruḷi ārāyumpōtu, viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi naṭuvu  niliyāy ārāytal vēṇṭum.
kāytal uvattal akaṟṟi oruporuṭkaṇ
āytal aṟivuṭiyār kaṇṇatē - kāyvataṉkaṇ
uṟṟa kuṇantōṉṟā tākum uvappataṉkaṇ
kuṟṟamun tōṉṟāk keṭum.	(aṟaneṟi: 42)
mēlum, vaṭacol teṉcoṟkaḷip pirittuṇartaṟku moḻinūl, Fyüš(Ethnology), carittira nūl mutaliya nūlkaḷiṉ tuṇi iṉṟiyami yātu vēṇṭappaṭum. ākiyāl, avaṟṟiṉ  tuṇikoṇṭu uṇmi yaṟintu kaṭippiṭikka. iṉṟēṟ kuñcaran taṭaviya kuruṭar kūṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉka.
piṟaṉpaḻi kūṟuvāṉ taṉpaḻi yuḷḷum
tiṟaṉterintu kūṟap paṭum. 	(kuṟaḷ. 186)
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' naḷi 1939
8
`māṇavaṉ' teṉcollā? vaṭacollā?
tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku muntiyamiyālum, āriyam nāvalan tēyattiṟ pukumuṉṉarē tamiḻ muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭintiruntamiyālum, intiya nākarikattiṉ cāramāṉa pakuti tamiḻ nākarikamē yātalālum, tamiḻuk kiṉṟiyamiyāta paḻañ coṟkaḷellām taṉittamiḻē eṉpatu muḻu vuṟutiyāṉa uṇmiyākum. āyiṉum, tamiḻaṉiṉ tāḻvuṇarcciyālum, ārāycciyiṉmiyālum, āriya ēmāṟṟattālum iṉṉum tamiḻiṉ perumi tamiḻarālum aṟiyappaṭavilli.
teṉcollā vaṭacollā eṉṟu aiyuṟappaṭum coṟkaṭku, kīḻkkāṇum nilimikaḷiṟ pala vottiruppiṉ teṉcolleṉṟum, irāviṭiṉ vaṭacolleṉṟum tuṇiyalām.
1.	vērum vērpporuḷum tamiḻiliruttal.
2.	kiḷiccoṟkaḷum vaḻiccoṟkaḷum, tamiḻil maṭṭu māyiṉum tamiḻilum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilumāyiṉum, ciṟu peruṅ kulamākavō kuṭumpamākavō iruttal.
3.	caṅkanūlkaḷil āṭcipeṟṟiruttal, allatu toṉṟu toṭṭa ulakavaḻakkil vaḻaṅkivaral.
4.	tamiḻeḻuttukaḷāl (tamiḻolikaḷāl) amintiruttal.
5.	nāṭṭuppuṟattuk kallāmakkaḷāl perumpāṉmiyākavō ciṟappākavō vaḻaṅkappeṟal.
6.	(aiyuṟappaṭum collāl) kuṟikkappeṟum poruḷ allatu karuttut tamiḻnāṭṭil allatu tamiḻar uḷḷat til toṉṟutoṭṭē (aḵtāvatu āriyar varumuṉ piruntē) iruntuvaral.
7.	colvaṭivu allatu collamiti tamiḻukkēṟṟal.
 iṉi, māṇavaṉ eṉṉum collukkuc ciṟuvaṉ eṉpatē vērp poruḷām. makkaḷ āciriyaṉiṭattuk kaṟkum paruvam ciṟuparuvamātalāl, ciṟuvaṉik kuṟikkum collē kaṟpōṉiyum kuṟittatu. iṉṟum, piḷḷikaḷ eṉṟum, paḷḷip piḷḷikaḷ eṉṟum māṇavar aḻikkap peṟutal kāṇka.
āṅkilattilum, ciṟuvaṉik kuṟikkum cila coṟkaḷ māṇavaṉi yuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
Pupil.n. (L. pupillus, pupilla, dim.s. of pupas, a boy; pupa, a girl.) 1. A young person of either sex under the care of an instructor or tutor; a scholar; a disciple.	
	-The Imperial Dictionary, p. 573.
Pedagogue. n. (Gk. pidagogus-pis, pidos, a child and ago, to lead') A teacher of children; one whose occupation is to instruct young children; a school master now used generally by way of contempt							      Ibid. p. 396.
māṇavaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ aṭi (Stem) maṇ eṉpatākum. iv vaṭiyiṉ vēr (Root) muḷ eṉpatu. iv vēriṉ iyalpuvaṭivam mul.
mul eṉpatu, ciṟumiyi allatu iḷamiyi uṇarttum vērc coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.

	3. ciṟukuḻanti.

``yāṭum kutiriyum navviyum uḻiyum
ōṭum pulvāy uḷappaṭa maṟiyē.''	(tol. 1511)

muṭṭuk kurumpi = ciṟu kurumpi. muṭṭu - muṭṭi = ciṟu karaṇṭi.
muṭṭi = moṭṭi. moṭṭip payal = ciṟu payal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭukku = ciṟu cantu.
moḻivaḷarcciyil coṟkaḷ aṭiyum palvakit tiripukaḷuḷ, iṉattiripu eṉpatu oṉṟu. atil uyiriṉat tiripu eṉpatu oru pirivu.  ōruyir maṟṟō ruyirākattirivatu uyiriṉat tiripām. enta vuyirum vēṟenta uyirākavum tiriyalām. āyiṉum, perumpāṉmiyāka iyalkiṉṟa cila muṟippaṭṭa uyiriṉat tiripukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ       u-a eṉpatu oṉṟu.

	kuṭṭi - kaṭṭi	muṟi - maṟi (=vaḷi)

maḷ - maḻa

ātalāl, maḻ eṉṉum vaṭivam tōṉṟātu,

``cāṭiyiṉ vaḻiccaṟa viḷinta'' (perumpā. 280)
putumi ciṟumi aḻaku vīram mutaliyaṉa iḷamiyoṭu toṭar puṭiya karuttukaḷ. ataṉāl, iḷamiyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ ip poruḷkaḷi uṇarttiṉa.
maḻali - matali = kuḻanti moḻi, kuḻanti, makaṉ.
matalikkiḷi = iḷaṅkiḷi.
