maṭami =	1. meṉmi. ``teḷinaṭi maṭappiṇi'' (puṟam. 23)
		2. aṟiyāmi
aṟivu perumpālum āṇṭup perukkattāl aṭiyap peṟuvatāl, iḷamiyil aṟiyāmi mikuntiruppatu iyalpu. itaṉāl, iḷamiccol aṟiyāmiyi uṇarttiṟṟu.
maḷ - (maṇ) - maṇi = ciṟiyatu.
maṇikkayiṟu, maṇikkāṭi, maṇikkuṭal, maṇikki, maṇikkōri, maṇiccampā, maṇiccuṟā, maṇittakkāḷi, maṇittutti,  maṇippayal, maṇippuṉku, maṇippuṟā, maṇippoccam, maṇiyīral mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
maṇ - māṇ -	1, ciṟuvaṉ, iḷiñaṉ, maṇavātāṉ (piramacāri).
	``māṇāki viya maḷantatuvum'' (tiv. periyatiru moḻi; 8 : 10 : 8,)
	2. kuṟaḷ, kuṟaḷaṉ.
	``kuṟumā ṇuruvaṉ taṟkuṟiyāk koṇṭāṭum''
	(tēvāram 164 : 5)
	māṇ - māṇi = 1. maṇavātāṉ, māṇavaṉ
	``karumāṇi yāyiranta kaḷvaṉē'' (tiv.iyaṟpā. 2:61)
	2. kuṟaḷ vaṭivam
	(tiv, periyāḻvār tirumoḻi, 1 : 4 : 1 svāpatēca viyākkiyāṉam).
	3. ciṟṟuṟuppu.
māṇi eṉṉum col kaṉṉaṭattilu muḷatu. māṇ, māṇi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi lilli.
māṇ - māṇavaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ, kaṟpōṉ.
māṇavaṉ - māṇavakaṉ = iḷiñaṉ, kaṟpōṉ, maṇavātāṉ, ciṟuvaṉ, aṟivīṉaṉ.
yāḻppāṇa akarātiyil, māṇavakaṉ eṉṉum collukku 8 mutal 16 āṇṭukkuṭpaṭṭa ciṟuvaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷatu.
potuvākak kalvikaṟkum paruvam maṇattiṟku muntiyatātalālum, maṇapparuvam āṭavaṉukkup patiṉāṟāṇṭeṉṟu akapporu ḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvatālum, 8 āṇṭu mutal 16 āṇṭuvarippaṭṭa iḷiñaṉi māṇavakaṉ eṉṟu aḻippatu tamiḻmarapiṟkē mikap poruttamām. iṉṟum, vacatiyuḷḷa āṭavarkkellām patiṉāṟām āṇṭilēyē maṇam nikaḻkiṉṟatu. āriyamaṇam eṭṭaṉuḷ oṉṟāṉa piramam eṉpatu, ``nāṟpatteṭṭiyāṇṭu piramacariyaṅ kāttavaṉukkup paṉṉīrāṭṭip paruvattāḷāyp pūppu eytiyavaḷip peyarttu iraṇṭām pūppu eytāmuṉ aṇikalaṉaṇintu tāṉamākak koṭuppatu'' (tol. poruḷ. 92, uri) tamiḻaṉ 48 āṇṭuvari maṇavātiruttal mika aritākum. veppanāṭṭār muntiyum taṭpanāṭṭār pintiyum maṇattal iyaṟki viḷivākum. iṉṟum, mēṉāṭṭār perumpālum nāṟpatiyāṇṭu nirampiya piṉṉarē maṇakkiṉṟaṉar. intiya āriyariṉ muṉṉōr ātiyil vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkumuḷḷa kuḷirnāṭukaḷiṟ kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, avarkku nāṟpatiyāṇṭu maṇavātirukka muṭintatu. tamiḻarkkō in nili aṉṟumilli; iṉṟumilli ātalāl, iḷaṅkaṟṟuccolliyi māṇavakaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻvaḻakkēyākum.
māṇavakam = kalvi (cintāmaṇi nikaṇṭu)
māṇavaṉ - (māṇakaṉ) - (māṇakkaṉ) - māṇākkaṉ.
māṇavi māṇākki eṉpaṉa peṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
māṇavaṉ māṇavakaṉ eṉṉum īr āṇ peyarum vaṭamoḻiyil, muṟiyē, māṇava māṇavaka eṉa īṟukeṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. māṇavikā eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peṇpāṟ peyar. māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāl vaṭivamum māṇavi  māṇākki eṉṉum peṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷum māṇavaṉ eṉpataṟku aṭivaḻiyāṉa koṭivaḻic coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyi lilli. ṉakaravīṟṟut tamiḻp peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ perumpālum īṟukeṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu iyalpu.
tolkāppiyattiṟku āttirēyaṉ pērāciriyaṉ vakutta potup pāyirattil,
``iṉṉō raṉṉa toṉṉeṟi marapiṉar
palperuñ ciṟappiṉ nallā ciriyar
....................................................
uṭiyō rāki naṭiyaṟin toḻukunar
naṉmā ṇākkareṉpa.....................''

eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āciriyaṉ, māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭum taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷākum.
māṇavaṉ, māṇavakaṉ, māṇākkaṉ eṉṉum mūvaṭivaṅkaḷum, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷālēyē amintavi.
āriyar varumuṉpē tamiḻar kalviyil tuṟipōyiruntaṉar. āciriya māṇava marapil kalvi vaḻaṅkivantatu.
``uṭiyārmuṉ illārpōl ēkkaṟṟuṅ kaṟṟār
kaṭiyarē kallā tavar.'' 	(kuṟaḷ. 395)
``cevikkuṇa villāta pōḻtu ciṟitu
tyiṟṟukkuÛyapgṭum.''		(Fṟaḷ.412)
eṉpavi piṟkālattaviyāyiṉum, muṟkāla nilimiyiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka. iṉi,
``otta cūttiram urippiṉ kāṇṭiki
meyppaṭak kiḷanta vakiya tāki'' 	(tol. 1598)
``cūttirat tuṭporuḷ aṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
iṉṟi yamiyā tiyipavi yellām
oṉṟa urippatu uriyeṉap paṭumē.'' 	(tol. 1603)
mutaliya pakutikaḷum, muṟkāṭṭiya potuppāyirap pakutiyum, āciriya vāyiṟ kalviyi uṇarttutal kāṇka. āciriya māṇavar āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭikkālattē jmiḻnāṭṭiliruntatiṉālmvarikFṟittaṟkucbcāṟkaḷumgḻantamiḻilïruntiruttalnvaṇṭum.f‰ngh‹, koḷvōṉ, kōḷāḷaṉ, kēṭpōṉ mutaliya māṇavaṉ bpayarkaḷum;fṟpippōṉ,Evalvōṉ,crippōṉKtaliyaMciriyaṉbpayarfḷum;Nttiraṅkaḷilumbcayyuḷkaḷilumnmāṉiyetukiknkaṟṟavāṟummikkappeṟumbpātucbcāṟkaḷēyaṉṟi,krapubnaṟippaṭṭaáṟappucbcāṟkaḷalla.f‰W¢brhšÈ eṉpatu iṭikkālatteḻunta toṭarmoḻip         peyar. māṇavaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ amippum tamiḻmuṟippaṭṭatē. māṇ, pakuti; avaṉ, vikuti. ūṉavaṉ, kāṉavaṉ, vāṉavaṉ, mīṉavaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka. ivaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷi orukāl ūṉam, kāṉam mutaliya makara vīṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷākak koḷḷiṉum, āṉavaṉ kōṉavaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷi aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷa muṭiyāmi kāṇka.
āṉavar = iṭiyar (aka. ni.).
māṇavakaṉ eṉpatu māṇavaṉ eṉpataṉ virittal. māṇākkaṉ eṉpatu māṇavaṉ eṉpataṉ tirittal. māṇavaṉ - māṇavi, māṇākkaṉ - māṇākki eṉa āṇpāṟpeyar aṉṉīṟum, peṇpāṟpeyar ikaravīṟum peṟṟiruppatu, tamiḻukkuc ciṟappām.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, teṉcoṟku vitikkappaṭṭa eḻu nilimikaḷuḷ āṟu (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7) māṇavaṉ eṉṉum collukku ēṟṟal kāṇka.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacoṟkaḷē eṉṉum kuruṭṭuk koḷkiyi nampi, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa tuṇiyāṉē teṉcoṟkaḷi yellām vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉa mayaṅkaṟka. vaṭamoḻicceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum teṉcoṟkaḷ pallāyirakkaṇakkiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu māṇavaṉ eṉpatu. tamiḻil cīṭaṉ eṉṟu taṟpavamāki vaḻaṅkum ciṣya eṉṉum collē vaṭacollākum.
itaṉiyum itupōṉṟa piṟa vuṇmikaḷiyum uṇartaṟku, tamiḻiyum tamiḻariyum tamiḻnāṭṭiyum paṟṟiya varalāṟṟuṇmikaḷi aṟital vēṇṭum. yām aṇmiyil veḷiyiṭum mutaṟṟāymoḻi, vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu, mutal nākarikam mutaliya nūlkaḷuḷ avaṟṟip parakkak kaṇṭu teḷika.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' navampar 1949
9
eṉ peyar eṉcol?
moḻiyip poṟuttavariyil nāṉ maṟimaliyaṭikaḷi muṟṟum aṭiyoṟṟic celpavaṉāyiruntum, tamiḻp pērāciriyar palarum moḻiyārāycciyiṉmiyāṉē eṉ peyariyum vaṭacolleṉa aiyuṟṟum varukiṉṟaṉar. avar mayakkaṟuppāṉ eḻuntatik kaṭṭuri.
1. tēvaṉ
ekkālattum māntar teyva vaḻipāṭāṟṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam, naṉmi ceyyum poruḷiṭattu aṉpum, tīmiceyyum poruḷiṭattu accamumē. ellām valla iṟivaṉ vaḻipāṭṭil iv viru meyppāṭukaḷum kalantuḷḷaṉa. iṟivaṉ iṟutiyil pēriṉpan taruvāṉ eṉpatu naṉmi yum, erinirayattil iṭuvāṉ eṉpatu tīmiyum paṟṟiya uṇarccikaḷākum.
veḷiyoḻinta nāṟpūtaṅkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum naṉmiyum tīmiyuñ ceyyumēṉum, avaṟṟuḷ tīyē avaṟṟic ciṟappāka allatu teḷivākac ceyvatām. ataṉāl, mutaṟkāla māntar ataṉiyē ciṟanta teyvamākap pōṟṟivantaṉar. atupaṟṟit teyvap potuppeyarum, muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭpeyarum tīppeyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭpulaṉip payaṉpaṭuvikkum oḷiyum, camiyaṟku vēṇṭum cūṭum, kuḷirip pōkkum vemmiyum, uṇavup payirkku vēṇṭum veppamum, tīyiṉāl uṇṭākum naṉmikaḷām; tīṇṭiṉāṟ cuṭuvatum, akappaṭiṉ erittuk kolvatum, ataṉāl viḷivum tīmikaḷām. nāṟ pūtaṅkaḷuḷḷum, ciṟṟaḷaviliruppiṉum tīmi ceyvatu tīyēyātaliṉ, tīmippeyar ataṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṟṟu.
marattoṭu maramum kalloṭu kallum uracumpōtum neriyum pōtum neruppuṇṭāvatik kaṇṭa mutaṟkāla māntar, avvaki yilēyē neruppuṇṭākkak kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl, uracal, nerical, tēytal mutaliya urāytaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu, tīyik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
uracu, urāy, urāyñcu, uriñ, uriñu, uriñcu, uri, uricu, uriñcu, urōcu, urōñcu eṉpaṉa oruvērp piṟanta oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ.
ur: ur - uru. uruttal = aḻalutal.
``ākam uruppa nūṟi'' (puṟam. 25 : 10)
uru - urum - urumpu = kotippu.
``urumpil kūṟṟat taṉṉa'' (patiṟṟup. 26 : 13).
uru - urumam = veppam. naṇpakal.
urumakālam = kōṭikālam.
urumittal = puḻuṅkutal,
uru - uruppu = veppam.
``kaṉmici uruppiṟak kaṉituḷi citaṟeṉa'' (kalit. 16 : 7)
uruppu - uruppam = veppam.
``kaṉalum................... uruppameḻa'' (aric. pu. vivā. 104)
uru - uruku - urukku - urukkam.
urukutal = veppattiṉāl iḷakutal, maṉam iḷakutal (iraṅkutal).
urukku = urukiṉa irumpu (eḵku).
urukkāṅkal = urukippōṉa ceṅkal.
ur - uri = neruppu (kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷuvam).
uri eṉṉum vaṭivam tamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟu maṟintatu.
uri - eri = neruppu.
neruppik kuṟikkum `uru' vēr tamiḻccoṟkaḷ piṟamoḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
ilattīṉ (Latin) - ur, (to burn)
	armīṉiyam (Armenian) ōr = neruppu.
	āpukkāṉiyam (Afghan) - ōr, vur = neruppu.
	epirēyam (Hebrew) ūr = neruppu, ōr = oḷi.
ner:	ner - neri - nerital = neruṅkutal, uracutal, noṟuṅkutal.
	ner - neru - neruppu. neruppu - nippu (teluṅku).
	arapiyam (Arabic) - nār = neruppu, nūr = oḷi.
tēy:	tēytal = uracutal.
	tēy - tīy - tī = neruppu, viḷakku, narakam.
	tī - tīmi = tīyiṉtaṉmi. tī. tīya = koṭiya,
	tīytal = erintupōtal, karukutal, paṟṟippōtal,
	tītal = tīytal.
ēkāram īkāramāyt tiritali, tēṉ - tēm - tīm eṉṉum tiripilum kaṇṭukoḷka.
tēy - tēyu (camaṟkirutam) = neruppu.
it `tēy' aṭiyiṉiṉṟē teyvappeyar tōṉṟiṟṟu.
tēy - tēyvu - tēvu - tēvaṉ,
tēyvu - teyvu - teyvam.
tēvu - tē = teyvam, talivaṉ,
``pālvari teyvam viṉiyē pūtam'' (tol. 541)
``teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟi'' (tol. 964).
``teyvam añcal puriyaṟan teḷital'' (tol. 1218)
``vaḻipaṭu teyvam niṟpuṟaṅ kāppa'' (tol. 1367).
yakaravoṟṟuḷḷa teyvap peyarē perumpāṉmiyākavum, 
``tēvarp parāaya muṉṉilik kaṇṇē'' (tol. 1395)
eṉa vakaravoṟṟuḷḷa piṉṉi vaṭivu arukiyum, itupōtuḷḷa tamiḻnūlkaḷuḷ muntiyatākiya tolkāppiyattuḷ varutal kāṇka.
camaṟkirutam - deva, diva
ilattīṉ - dues = god.
kirēkkam (Greek) - theos = god.
`tēva' eṉṉum coṟkuc camaṟkirutattiṟ kāṭṭappaṭum div (to be bright). di, dip (to shine) eṉṉum vērccoṟkaḷ mutaṟporuḷaṉṟi vaḻipporuḷē koṇṭuḷḷamiyiṉ poruntāmi kāṇka.
piṉvarum cittiya (Scythian) āriyac coṟkaḷ, `tī' (neruppu) eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟku iṉamāṉavi eṉak kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kāṭṭuvār.
cittiyam:
cāmāyitē (samoiede) - tu, tui, ti, ty.
mañcu (Manchu) - tua.
aṅkēriyam (Hungarian) - tuz.
ōcuttiyakku (Ostiak) - tut.
tuṅkacu (Tungus) - togo.
ilacukkiyam (Lesghian) - tze, zi, zie.
āriyam :
	kēlikkam (Gaelic) - teire
vēlicu (Welsh) - tan
pāracīkam (Persian) - tigh
2. nēyaṉ
naḷ :	naḷ - naṇ - naṇpu - naṭpu.
naḷḷutal = aṭital, poruntutal, naṭpuk koḷḷutal, ceṟital.
naḷḷār = pakivar.
naḷḷi = uṟavu (cūṭā.).
naḷḷiruḷ = ceṟintaviruḷ (tirukkō. 156, cilap. 15 : 105)
naḷḷunar = naṇpar (tivā.).
naḷ - naḷi. naḷital = ceṟital.
naḷ - neḷ - ney = oṭṭum nīrpporuḷ (oil, ghee),
	karuppukkaṭṭic cāntu.
neytal = iṇittal, nūli iṇittu āṭiyākac ceytal.
ney = kuruti, neyttōr (neyttuvar?) = kuruti,
akaram ekaramātali, paru - peru, cattāṉ - cettāṉ eṉṉum tiripukaḷiṟ kāṇka.
ḷakara meyyīṟu yakara meyyīṟāvati, koḷ - koy, toḷ - toy, poḷ - poy mutaliya tiripukaḷiṟ kāṇka.
ney - (neyñcu) - neñcu - neñcam = vilāvelumpukaḷ iṇikkappaṭṭa iṭam allatu aṉpiṟkiṭamākak karutappaṭum neñcāṅkuli (Heart) uḷḷa iṭam.
ney - nēy - nēyam = aṉpu.
īram, paci mutaliya coṟkaḷ oṭṭum poruḷiyum aṉpiyum kuṟittal kāṇka. aṉpu, iruvari allatu palari iṇippatu.
nēy - nē = aṉpu. nēyam - nēcam.
ya - ca. o.nō: neyavu - necavu.
nēcam - nēci. nēcittal = aṉpu kaṭāttal virumputal.
``nēcittu racavāta vittik kalintiṭuvar'' (tāyumāṉavar)
nēyam - nēyaṉ. nēcam - nēcaṉ.
nēyam eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭattil vaḻaṅki, piṉṉarp pirākirutattilum toṭarntu, iṟutiyil camaṟkirutattiṟ pukuntu `nēha' eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iv varalāṟṟumuṟi aṟiyātār, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟāṉ eṉpatupōl, nēha (camaṟ) - nēyam (pirākirutam) - nēyam (tamiḻ) eṉat talikīḻāyt tirippar. iṅṅaṉamē ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyilum, uṇmikku māṟākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pakuttaṟivum naṭuvunilimiyumuḷḷa aṟiñar uṇmi kaṇṭukoḷvārāka. nēyam vaṭacollāyiṉ `ney' eṉpatum ataṉ aṭivērāṉa `naḷ' eṉpatum vaṭacollātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamākāmi veḷḷiṭimali pōl teḷḷitē.
vaṭamoḻi āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇ ṭiruppatāl, moḻinūṉ muṟippaṭi naṭuvuniliyāy ārāyntu uṇmi kāṇavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyātalāl piṟamoḻiyi ṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉkoḷḷāteṉṉum karunāṭakak kuruṭṭuk koḷkiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, tūya teṉcoṟkaḷiyum vaṭacolleṉa valippatu, aṟivārāycci mikka ikkālattiṟ kēṟkāteṉa, vaṭamoḻi vāṇar tiṭamāka aṟivārāka; ārāycciyillāta tamiḻp pērāciriyarum, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa collellām vaṭacolleṉak karutum pēti miyi viṭṭuyvārāka. mayir eṉṉum teṉcol `cmacru' (smrsru) eṉac cakaram mutaṟkoṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatupōṉṟē, nēyam eṉṉum teṉ collum `nēha' eṉa sakaram muṟkoṇṭu camaṟkirutattil vaḻaṅku kiṉṟateṉa aṟika.
- ``teṉmoḻi'' navampar 1959
10
cili eṉṉuñ col varalāṟu
uruvam, cili, paṭimi eṉṉum oruporuḷ muccoṟkaḷuḷ mutalatum īṟṟatum aiyuṟavaṟṟa tūya teṉcoṟkaḷ.
uruvam
uru eṉṉum vaṭivum uruvattik kuṟikkum.
urukkal = kōvil kaṭṭiyavaruṭiya kaṟcili. itu kaṭṭaṭak kali vaḻakku.
uruvam = cili (piratimi).
uruvāram = cili (piratimi) itu ulaka vaḻakku.
uruvam eṉpatu rūpa eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripeṉpar. itu ēmāṟṟu.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal.
uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, vaṭivuṭip poruḷ, paṭimi.
uru - uruvu - urupu =vēṟṟumi vaṭivam; atik kuṟikkum eḻuttu allatu aci allatu col.
uruvam eṉṟa teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil rūpa eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
paṭimi
paṭi = oppu, vaki, uruvam (cīvaka. 1156). appaṭi =atupōl, avvaki. orupaṭiyāy (vakiyāy) varukiṟatu. oru vaṭiyāy eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku.
paṭiyōli = mūla motta ōli.
paṭi = prati (va.) itiyum prati > paṭi eṉat talikīḻāyk kāṭṭi ēmāṟṟuvar vaṭavar.
paṭi - paṭimi - pratimā (va.) - patumi (taṟpavam).
paṭimi - paṭimam = nōṉpuk kōlam, tavavaṭivam.
(paṭi - paṭivu - paṭivam; paṭivu - vaṭivu; paṭivam - vaṭivam.)
cili
cili eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyil kal allatu pāṟi eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. Silex eṉṉum ilattīṉ collum, ataṉiṉṟu tirinta silica eṉṉum āṅkilac collum vaṉkalli (second stone) yaṉṟi uruvac ciliyik kuṟiyā. nāmtāṉ atik karuviyākupeyarāy uruvam eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
cilā cācaṉam = kallil eḻutappaṭṭa kaṭṭaḷi, kaṟpaṭṭayam, vikkirakam, mūrttam eṉpaṉavē uruvattik kuṟikkum vaṭacoṟkaḷ. mūrttam uṭiyavaṉ mūrtti. pratimā eṉpatu teṉcoṟṟiripu.
nām kaṟcili eṉpatupōl vaṭavar cilācilā eṉṉa muṭiyātu iruppuccili, veḷḷiccili, ceppuccili, poṟcili, veṇkalac cili eṉṟu uruvapporuḷiltāṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. kaṟporuḷil eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatilli. teyva uruvam teyvaccili eṉappaṭum.
vaṭamoḻiyil kal eṉṉum poruṭkum vēr allatu mutaṉili illi. māṉiyar villiyamcu tam akaramutaliyil, orukāl `ci' mūlamāyirukkalām eṉpar. `ci' eṉpataṟku nalku, pontiki (tirupti)p paṭuttu, kūrākku eṉṉum poruḷkaḷtām uṇṭu. avi poruntā. tamiḻilum tiṭṭamākavō, tēṟṟamākavō mūlaṅkāṭṭutaṟ killi. cili eṉṉuñ coṟkuc cil eṉpatutāṉ mūlamāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
cil = tuṇṭu, vaṭṭam, vaṭṭapporuḷ.
cilleṉal = kuḷirtal, olittal.
cil - cili = vaḷinta vil, olikkum etiroli.
cil - cilampu = olikkum kaḻal (paral iṭappaṭṭa kālaṇi), etiroliyiṭum mali, olikkum cilampak kaḻi. (cilampu - cilampam).
cilittal = olittal, muḻaṅkutal, āravārittal - `malipaṭukaṭām' ikkaruttatu. pāṭumpōtu etirolittalic ciliyōṭutal eṉpar nelli nāṭṭār.
cil eṉpatu vaṭṭam eṉṉum karuttil cul eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
culavutal = vaḷital. tuṇṭu eṉṉum karuttutāṉ cil eṉpatic ciliyōṭu iṇikka ōraḷavu utavum.
cil - cilukku = veṭṭiṉa tuṇṭu; cilukku - citukku - cetukku.      (la - ta pōli) o.nō: calaṅki - cataṅki; kalampam - katampam; mel - metu.
cil - cili = veṭṭiṉa allatu cetukkiṉa uruvam. ātalāl, kal eṉṉum poruḷ tamiḻil illi.
iṉi, cili eṉpatu cilampu eṉpatu pōl mali eṉṟum tamiḻil poruḷ koṇṭiruppatāl, mali eṉṉum poruḷiṉiṉṟē kal (malippiñcu) eṉṉum mutalākupeyarp poruḷum teṉmoḻiyilum, vaṭamoḻiyilum tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
cili = mali - ``vaṇaṅkāc ciliaḷitta tōḷāṉ'' (pu.ve. 2:12)
cili = kal (piṅka.)
ākavē, cili eṉṉum uruvapporuṭ coṟkut tamiḻ mūlam caṟṟu valintu koḷvatākavē iruppiṉum, piṉvarum ētukkaḷāl atit teṉcol eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
1.	vaṭamoḻiyil cili eṉṉum coṟku mūlamilli.
2.	am moḻiyil ac coṟkuk kaṟporuḷēyaṉṟi uruvap poruḷilli. tamiḻil iraṇṭumuṇṭu.
3.	āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉpē tamiḻakattil uruvak kali iruntatu.
4.	vaṭamoḻiyil kuṟintapakkam aintiliru pakuti teṉcol. (eṉ 	`vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu' pārkka.)
5.	uruvam, paṭimi eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷum vaṭacollākak kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi pōṉṟē cili eṉpatum.
6.	vēta āriyarkku vēḷvi vaḻipāṭēyaṉṟi uruva vaṇakkamilli. teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉṉarē tamiḻariṭamiruntu mēṟkoṇṭaṉar.
7.	cilā eṉṉum vaṭacollil irumoḻip potuveḻuttē yaṉṟi vaṭamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttilli.
8.	tamiḻ aikāravīṟu vaṭamoḻiyil ākāra īṟāyt tirivatu iyalpē. e-ṭu: māli - mālā; vaṭi - vaṭā.
9.	cilā eṉṉuñ col irukku vētattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa vaṭanūlkaḷilēyē āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
10.	cilampu, cili eṉṉum iru (malipporuṭ) coṟkaḷum, tamiḻil cil eṉṉum orē mūlattiyum, olittal eṉṉum orē vērpporuṭ karaṇiyattiyum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
11.	tamiḻ mēli āriyattiṟkum aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷi utaviya peruvaḷamoḻi.
- nākit tamiḻccaṅka maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
niṉivu malar 1969
11
karumam tamiḻc collē!
karumam eṉpatu, kumarik kaṇṭattil karu eṉṉum mutaṉili yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟit toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkivaruvatum, aṭināḷil airōppā ceṉṟa tamiḻar al, tiraviṭar moḻiyiṟ kalantiruntatumāṉa tūya tamiḻc collākum.
karumam = ceyal, toḻil, kaṭami. R.V. karman
karu - karumi - karumam.
o.nō :	paru - parumi - parumam.
	uva - uvami - uvamam.
makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ tamiḻilum ṉakara vīṟākat tiritaluṇṭu.
e-ṭu : parumam - parumaṉ. uvamam - uvamaṉ.
karu-ttal = ceytal. itu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa tamiḻ viṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟām. aṭṭu, poṭṭu, kaḷ, naṉ mutaliya pala viṉikaḷ vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉami kāṇka.
aṭṭutal = vārttal
poṭṭutal = poruttutal, kaṭṭutal
poruttu - pottu - poṭṭu
poṭṭu + aṇam - poṭṭaṇam.
o.nō :	kaṭṭu + aṇam = kaṭṭaṇam
	kaṭṭal = kaḷavu ceytal. kaḷḷāmi = kaḷavu ceyyāmi.
	naṭṭal = naṭpuc ceytal.
oruporuḷ paṟṟip pala colliyiruppiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum cilavum vaḻaṅkappaṭāmaṟ pōtal iyalpē.
karu-ttal eṉṉum coṟku iruvakiyil vērp poruḷ kūṟalām.
1.	fUik = bgUik (âth.), mikuti. kaṭu - karu.
	kaṭuttal = mikutal. ``neñcaṅ kaṭuttaru'' (kuṟaḷ. 706)
	karuttal = muṉa illāta toṉṟu mikumāṟu ceytal, ceytal.
āṅkilattil make eṉṉum colliṟku, itē karuttil, magnus (L), megas (Gk.) eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ mag eṉṉum vēriyē mūlamākak kāṭṭutal kāṇka.
karumam - kammam. (> Pkt. kamma) = 1. kammiyar toḻil, smith's work `kammañ cey mākkaḷ' (nālaṭiyār, 393) 2.	uḻavut toḻil.
kammavār (kammam + avar) - uḻavut toḻil ceyyum oru teluṅka vakuppār.
kammam - kamam = uḻavut toḻil.
nākarika vulakil mutaṉ mutal ēṟpaṭṭa kittoḻil uḻavut toḻilē. ataṉāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar uḻavarik ``kiceytūṇ māli yavar'' (1035) eṉṟār.
kamam - kam =	1. uḻavut toḻil. 
	2. kammiyar toḻil, ``ī muṅ kammum'' (tol. eḻut. 328)
kammāli =	1. erutu pūṭṭiyirukkum iṟivi yēṟṟam.
	2. kaṉṉār toḻiṟkūṭam.
kammāli - kamali - kavali.
kamali eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku. kavali eṉpatu cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
kaṉṉaṭa teluṅku moḻikaḷil (ciṟappākak kaṉṉaṭattil, vakaram pakaramākat tirivatāl, kavali eṉpatu avviru moḻikaḷilum kapile (kapile) eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itik kurāṟ peṟṟattik kuṟikkum kapilā eṉṉum vaṭacollōṭu mayakki, (kavaliyēṟṟattik kapiliyēṟṟamākak koṇṭu) kapili eṉak kuṟittuḷḷaṉar ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyāḷar. mutalāvatu, kapiliyeṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil ōriṭattilum vaḻaṅkavilli. iraṇṭāvatu, kapili eṉpatu kapila niṟamuḷḷa peṟṟam (pacu.) kamaliyil kāḷikaḷik kaṭṭi nīriṟipparē yaṉṟip peṟṟaṅkaḷik kaṭṭi yiṟiyār.
kamali vaṇṭi, kamaliyuruḷi, kamalivaṭam, kamalikkuḻi mutaliya palacoṟkaḷ kamali yiṟiviyoṭu toṭarpuḷḷavi. ivaṟṟi mēṟkuṟitta akarātiyāciriyar aṟavum aṟintilar.
paṇṇutal eṉṉum collum itē karuttaṭippaṭiyil amintatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. paṇ - paṇṇi = kūṭṭam, peruṅkūṭṭam, tokuti, mikuti.
2. karuttal = ki karukkumāṟu oru viṉi ceytal, ceytal.
toṭarntu vēli ceyvataṉāl, karu niṟattār ki karuppatum poṉṉiṟattār allatu veṇṇiṟattār ki civappatum iyalpu. `ceyta ki cēvēṟum' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. cēvu - cēku = cevvayiram.
cey - cemmi. ceytal - ki civakkumāṟu oru viṉi ceytal, ceytal.
ivviruvaki mūlaṅkaḷuḷ etu uṇmiyāyiṉum, karumam eṉpatu tamiḻc collātal tēṟṟam. atu vaṭamoḻiyil tirinta mutaṉiliyē yaṉṟi vērp poruḷ ētumilli.
karu + vi = karuvi. oru toḻil ceyvataṟku vēṇṭum mūlapporuḷ allatu tuṇipporuḷ.
ic col tamiḻeṉpati evarum maṟukkār. itu tamiḻāyiṉ itaṉoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa karumam eṉpatum tamiḻēyātal vēṇṭum.
karu + aṇam = karaṇam = ceyal. maṇac ceyaṟcaṭaṅku, ceyaṟku vēṇṭum karuvi.
karaṇam - karaṇiyam = aṟivup pulak karuviyāṉa akakkaraṇam.         R.V. karana.
karaṇam eṉpati nīṭṭik kāraṇa eṉṟum, (ataṉoṭu poruntak) karu eṉṉum mutaṉiliyi nīṭṭik kārya eṉṟum, vittum viḷiyum pōlak karumamum paṟṟiya iru nuṇporuṭ coṟkaḷi amittuk koṇṭaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ivaṟṟit tamiḻiṟ cērttatiṉālēyē, karumam, karaṇam eṉṟum coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkutal paṟṟi vaṭacolleṉak karutappaṭṭaṉa. kāraṇam, kāriyam eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkum īṭāka, karaṇiyam, karumiyam eṉa iru coṟkaḷi amittuk koḷḷalām.
kammak kuṭam = kammiyar (kaṉṉār) ceyta kuṭam.
kam - kammi = kuyavaṉ. ``maṭkalañ cey kammi'' (pārata. tiru. 64) kalañ ceyyum oppumiyāl, kuyavaṉum kaṉṉāṉpōṟ karutappeṟṟāṉ.
kam - kammiyam = 1. kittoḻil; 2. kammāḷar toḻil.
kammiyam- kammiyaṉ =	1. toḻilāḷi
	``kammiyaru mūrvar kaḷiṟu'' (cīvaka. 495)
	2. kammāḷaṉ (tivā.)
	3. necavāḷi. ``kammiyar kuḻīi'' (maturik 521)
	4. poṟiviṉiñaṉ, mechanic
kam - kammāḷaṉ (Pkt. kammara) = kollaṉ, taccaṉ, ciṟpaṉ, taṭṭāṉ, kaṉṉāṉ eṉṉum aivakik kammiyaruḷ oru vakiyaṉ.
kammāḷaṉ - kammāṇaṉ. ``kammāṇa cēriyum'' (S.I.I. ii). 43
o.nō. : kaḷavāḷi - kaḷavāṇi, vaḷari - vaṇari.
kollaṉik kuṟikkum karumakaṉ eṉṉum col, karumam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirinta taṉiccol aṉṟu; irumpi yuṇarttum karu eṉṉum collum, āṭavaṉik kuṟikkum makaṉ eṉṉum collum cērnta kūṭṭuc collākum.
karumakaṉ - karumāṉ - karumaṉ - kollaṉ (piṅ.)
karuṅkol = irumpu (cūṭā.)
karuṅkollaṉ = karumāṉ. karumakaṉ = kollaṉ. ``karumakak kammiyaṉ'' (kampa. pampā. 37) karumam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu karumakaṉ eṉpatu tōṉṟiyiruppiṉ, `karumak kammiyaṉ' eṉṟu mikipaṭu collāṟ kuṟikka vēṇṭiyatilli.
iṉi, karumiyeṉṉuñ coṟku valimip poruḷum (puṟam. 2; cilap. 5, 29) iruppatāl, `karuṅkik kollaṉ' (cilap. 5, 29) eṉṉum toṭaric cāṉṟākak koṇṭu, karumakaṉ eṉpataṟku valiyavaṉ allatu vaṉ ceyalaṉ eṉṟu poruḷurikkavum iṭamuṇṭu. āyiṉum, aḵtu attuṇic ciṟappum porutta muṭiyataṉṟu.
karumāṉ eṉpatu, cemmāṉ (shoemaker) eṉpataṉoṭu niṟam paṟṟi muraṇpaṭṭatu. cemmakaṉ - cemmāṉ.
o.nō : blacksmith x white smith (worker in tin).
cemmāṉ eṉpati, Carman (S) eṉṉum tōṟ peyariṉ tiripākac ceṉṉit tamiḻakarāti kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum.
cf. E.	tan. to convert raw hide into leather, to make brown by exposure to sun.
oru kulattik kuṟikkumpōtu, palarpāṟ peyaroṭu akarac cāriyi cērttuk kuṟippatu tamiḻar vaḻakkam.
e-ṭu :	orumip peyar	paṉmippeyar	kulappeyar
	taṭṭāṉ	taṭṭār	taṭṭārakkulam
	cekkāṉ	cekkār	cekkārakkulam
	vaṇṇāṉ	vaṇṇār	vaṇṇārakkulam
immuṟippaṭi, karumāṉ colliṉiṉṟu karumārak kulam eṉpatu amiyum. iti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu kollaṉ peyar Karmara eṉṟu irukku vētattil amintatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ceytalik kuṟikkum karu eṉṉum viṉimutaṉili, vaṭamoḻi yil karu (kr) eṉṟum, ilattīṉil (cres) eṉṟum niṟkum. ōr iyalpāṉa vērccol uyir mutalāy allatu uyir meymmutalāy irukkumē yaṉṟi, meymmutalāy irātu. karma, karṇa, karttā, kāraṇa, kārya mutaliya tiricoṟkaḷellām, vaṭamoḻiyilum uyir meym mutalāyē yiruttal kāṇka.
12
etu tēvamoḻi?
ulakiluḷḷa (ēṟattāḻa) mūvāyira moḻikaḷuḷ, orucila, maṟi nūluṭimi paṟṟit tammit tūyamoḻi (Holy Language) eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷḷum camaṟkirutam taṉṉit tēvamoḻi (Divine Language) eṉṟum, pōṟṟip pukaḻntu koḷkiṉṟaṉa. tūyamoḻi eṉpatiṉum tēvamoḻi eṉpatu uyarvāṉatu. muṉṉatu maṇṇulakattil teyvat taṉmi yaṭinta makkaḷ moḻi yeṉṟum, piṉṉatu viṇṇulakatti ṉiṉṟō vīṭṭulakattiṉiṉṟō maṇṇulakattiṟku vanta tēvarmoḻi yeṉṟum, karuttup piṟappippaṉa.
ulakil oru moḻiyum tēvamoḻi yaṉṟu. oṉṟit tēvamoḻi yeṉṟu kuṟippiṉ, atu puṉinturivakiyākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam puṉinturi vakiyiṟ kuṟittaṟkum, kīḻk kuṟikkappeṟum kuṇaṅkaḷaṉittum ataṟkiruttal vēṇṭum.
(1)	ulakamoḻi mutaṉmi
(2)	oliyeḷimi
(3)	paṉmoḻit tāymi
(4)	oppuyarvaṟṟa paṇpāṭu
(5)	tūymi
(6)	maṟinūlum palkali ilakkiyamum uṇmi
(7)	makkaṭ potuvurimi
(8)	naṭunili aṟaṅkūṟal
iv veṇṇiyalpukaḷum oruṅkē tamiḻukkuḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭē camaṟkirutattiṟkuḷḷaṉa.
(1) vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉa vēta āriya moḻiyum akkālattu vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷākiya (tamiḻ uṭpaṭṭa) pirākiruta moḻikaḷum kalanta aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya moḻiyē vaṭamoḻi yeṉṟu ciṟappāyk kūṟappaṭum camaṟkirutamām. (ki.mu. 2000).
tamiḻō, māntaṉ mutal mutal tōṉṟiya (Lemuria eṉṉum) kumarināṭṭil, tāṉē tōṉṟiya iyaṟki moḻiyām (ki.mu. 50,000).
``ōṅka liṭivantu......... taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ,'' eṉṉum paḻiya taṉip pāvum itaṉip pulappaṭuttum.
(2) ``āṟṟal aḻiyumeṉ ṟantaṇarkaḷ nāṉmaṟiyip
pōṟṟiyurit tēṭṭiṉ puṟatteḻutār - ēṭṭeḻuti
vallunarum vallārum vaḷḷuvaṉār muppālic
colliṭiṉum āṟṟalcōr viṉṟu.''
eṉṟu kōtamaṉārum,
``ōtaṟ keḷitāy uṇartaṟ karitāki
............................................
vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻi māṇpu.''
eṉṟu māṅkuṭi marutaṉārum, kūṟiyaviyē. tamiḻoli meṉmikkum camaṟkirutavoli vaṉmikkum pōtiya cāṉṟām.
(3) tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaṭkellām tāyāyiruppatu pōṉṟē, camaṟkirutam vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaṭkellām tāy eṉṟoru tavaṟāṉa karuttu neṭuṅkālamāka nilavi varukiṉṟatu. oru kālattilum ulaka vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkāta aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiyak kalavimoḻi, eṅṅaṉam tāymoḻiyāyiruttal kūṭum? oru moḻi mutaṟkaṇ taṉimoḻiyā yiruntālaṉṟō piṉṉart tāymoḻiyāyum amital kūṭum! tamiḻ, tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkut tāyum, āriya moḻikaṭku mūlamumā yirukkumpōtu, av vāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oliyaḷavil mutuvaḷarcci yaṭinta ilakkiyac ceyaṟkik kalavi moḻiyākiya camaṟkirutam eṅṅaṉam uṇmiyil paṉmoḻit tāyāyiruttal ollum?
(4) ``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar tarukkumāṟu, ilakkaṇac cemmiyum vēṟem moḻiyiṉumillāta poruḷilakkaṇamumuṭiya tamiḻē moḻip paṇpāṭṭil taliciṟantatām.
(5) peruñ colvaḷa muṭiyatum piṟamoḻit tuṇi vēṇṭātatum taṉittu vaḷarntōṅka vallatum tamiḻ oṉṟē,
``aṉṟiyum tamiḻnūṟ kaḷavili avaṟṟuḷ
x‹nw ahÆD« jÅ¤jÄ G©nlh?''
eṉṉum cuvāmināta tēcikar kūṟṟu. moḻiyārāycci yillāta iruṭkālat teḻuntatātaliṉ, atu iṟṟikkup poruḷaṟṟateṉa viṭukka.
camaṟkirutattiṟkuḷḷa colvaḷam paṉmoḻic cērkkiyāl uṇṭāṉatātaliṉ, atuvum ciṟappilateṉṟu viṭukka.
(6) ``pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē''	(1336)
   ``maṟimoḻi kiḷanta mantirat tāṉa''	(1421)
``niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa''	(1434)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyar kūṟṟum,
``ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icikaṇak kiratam cālam
tāraṇam maṟamē cantam tampanīr nilamu lōkam,
māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
vāraṇam koṇṭa tantō vaḻivaḻip peyarum māḷa''
eṉṉum paḻantaṉippāṭalum, paṇṭit tamiḻil maṟinūlum palkali ilakkiyamum iruntu piṉṉar iṟantupaṭṭamiyi uṇarttum. paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam iṟantupaṭṭamiyiṉālēyē, ataṉ moḻipeyarppāṉa camaṟkiruta ilakkiyam iṉṟu mutal tōṉṟiya mūlamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.
(7) āriya vētattiyum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa camaṟkiruta nūl kaḷiyum tamiḻarāṉa cūttirar ōtiṉ, avar nāvi aṟuttuviṭa vēṇṭu meṉṟum, avaṟṟik kēṭpiṉ avar kātil urukkiṉa īyatti vārttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, āriya aṟanūlkaḷ aṟikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiyum tamiḻ nūlkaḷik kaṟṟaṟkum kēṭṭaṟkumō ettakiya kulamata kaṭciyiṉa vilakkum vēṟupāṭumilli.
``ceyyā moḻikkum tiruvaḷ ḷuvarmoḻinta
poyyā moḻikkum poruḷoṉṟē - ceyyā
ataṟkuriyar antaṇarē ārāyiṉ ēṉi
itaṟkuriyar allātār il''
eṉṟu veḷḷivītiyārum pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka.
(8) āriya aṟanūlkaḷ yāvum kaṭukaḷavum naṭuniliyaṟiyātavi yeṉpatu, avi kulattiṟkoru muṟiyum taṇṭamum kūṟuvatiṉiṉṟu teḷivāy aṟiyappaṭum.
``vaḷḷuvarcey tirukkuṟaḷi maṟuvaṟanaṉ kuṇarntōrkaḷ
uḷḷuvarō maṉuvāti orukulattiṟ korunīti''
eṉṉum cuntarampiḷḷi vāyuri īṇṭuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ pallāṟṟāṉum taliciṟanta moḻiyāyum, civaneṟiyum tirumālneṟiyum tūyatamiḻ mataṅkaḷāyum, iruppavum, tamiḻ potuvaḻipāṭṭiṟkut takāta moḻiyeṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭapiṉṉum, tamiḻar āriyariṉum tāḻntavareṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭapiṉṉum, tamiḻum tamiḻilakkiyamum iḻappiṟkum aḻippiṟkum iṭaṉāy, iṟṟic ciṟumiyaṭintuḷḷaṉa. aṟivum ārāycciyum mikka ikkālattilēṉum, tamiḻar viḻitteḻuntu tam muṉṉōr nilimiyaṭiya muyalvārāka.
- ``teṉmoḻi'' cepṭampar 1959
13
camaṟkirutavākkam - coṟkaḷ
vēta āriyar nāvalantēyam allatu nāvalampoḻil eṉṉum intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu, iṅkirunta ilakkiyam tamiḻilakkiyamē. avar intiya nākarikam tamateṉak kāṭṭit tammik kumukāya (camutāya) niliyil ucca nilikku uyarttuvāṉ vēṇṭi, tamiḻnūlkaḷi moḻi peyarttu ōr ilakkiyatti amittaṟkup pōtiya colvaḷam tam muṉṉōr moḻikkiṉmiyāl āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭaṉ koṇ ṭaminta ilakkiyac ceyaṟkik kalavi moḻiyē camaṟkirutam. itaṟku, ēṟkeṉavē iruvēṟu nilikaḷil nērallā vaḻiyiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭ ṭirunta tamiḻccoṟkaḷum tuṇiceytaṉa. itaṉāl, kīḻiyāriyam mūvēṟu kālattil tamiḻccoṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇṭami aṟiyappaṭum.
avaṟṟuḷ:
mutaṟkālam mēliyāriyak kālamākum. ak kālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavi mutaṟ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the First Period) eṉat takum.
iraṇṭāṅ kālam vēta āriyak kālamākum. akkālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavi iraṇṭāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Second Period) eṉat takum.
mūṉṟāṅ kālam camaṟkirutak kālamākum. ak kālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavi mūṉṟāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Third Period) eṉattakum.
1. mēliyāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
e-ṭu:
tiyūttāṉiyam:
amma - amma, āṉ ( = aṅku) - yon, iru - are, irum(pu) - iron, īṉ - yean, ean; uraṟu - roar, kari - cry, kāṇ - con, cun, can; kuraṅku   (= vaḷi) - crank, kuruḷ - curl, koṭukku - crook, cappu - sup, sip;       turuva - through, nāku - nag, paṭṭu (aṭi) - beat, pullam - bull, metu - smooth, muṉṉu - mun (think), vēṇṭu - want.
ilattīṉam
amar - amo (love), amor; uruḷi - uruṇṭa - rota (wheel), uri - or, kummal - cumulus, camam - similis, cēr - ser, tā - do, nāvāy - navis, paṭaku - barca, puri - par (equal), maṉ - man (remin), mutir - maturus, mel - mellow (soft), val - val (strong), vari (vaḷi) - vers (turn).
kirēkkam
aracaṉ - arthos, arkhon, kuru (niṟam) - chroma, kōṇam - gona, cāyi (niḻal) - skia, taccaṉ - tekton, tiruppu - trepos, turappaṇam - trapanon, toli - telos, narampu - neuron, nīram - neros (wet), pala - polus, pari - peri (round), paṟaḻ - proles, pāḻi (nakaram) - polis, puri - poros, mattiki - mastix, mastigos, māl - melas (black), viḻi - philos.
potu
pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēliyāriyattil pala moḻikaṭku allatu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaṭkup potuvākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
a. (cēymic cuṭṭu) - E. a (that), Du. a (dat). Ger. a (das).
i. (aṇmic cuṭṭu) - E. i (this) OE. (hit), Goth (hita), L. (hic).
ī. (aṇmic cuṭṭu) - E. (these).
itōḷ (ivviṭam) - E. hither. OE. hider, L. citra.
(iy) - iyal - L. i, e; Gk. i ei; Goth. i. Slav. i, Lith. ei.
iyalutal = iyaṅkutal, naṭattal, cellutal. uy(iy) - iya - iyaṅku. iya - iyavu = celavu, vaḻi. iya - iyavuḷ = vaḻi. uytal = cellutal. uyttal = celuttutal.
ēr (kalappi). E ear., L. ar.
oṉ - oṉṉu, oṉṟu - E. one. Du. een, Ger. ein, L. unus, Gk. oinos.
kotti = pūṉi (kaṉṉaṭac col). pakaliṟ kuruṭu pōliruppatu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. L. catta; E.cat.
cāli - Ger. saal, It. sala, salone, F. salon, E. saloon, E.hall Du. and Dan. hal.
pati - patam - pātam. nilattiṟ pativatu patam allatu pātam. pātam - pāti = vaḻi. patam = vaḻi. aṭittaṭam toṭarntu pativataṉāl oru vaḻi amiyum. aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ aṭippākam. atu, kāl, pātam, kīḻiṭam mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷi yuṇarttum. pātam eṉpatu nilattiṟ patiyakkūṭiya paranta aṭippakuti. pātam vitta viḷakku eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. aṭimaram paruttatu, marattaṭiyil uṭkārntāṉ, mēṭikkaṭiyiṟ pār, aṭināḷiliruntu collivarukiṉṟēṉ, eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷil pātam eṉṉum col poruntāmi kāṇka.
patam, pātam - Gk. podos. L. pedis. OE.fot (E.foot), Ger. fuss.
pāti - E. path, OE. peth, Dut. pad, Ger. pfad.
bghW - OE., OHG. ber., Goth. bir, E.bear, L.fer, Gk. pher.
pōṭu - E.put, pot. pyt; Dan. putte. L. pon.
pōkaviṭu - pōkku - pōṭu.
iṅkuk kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷuḷ, kīḻiyāriyattil illātaṉavu muḷa. avi mēliyāriyattiṉiṉṟu pirintu varumpōtu kīḻiyāriyattāl iḻakkappaṭṭaṉavākum.
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēliyāriyattiṉum kīḻiyāriyattil tirintiruppatu, piṉṉataṉ piṉmiyik kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
ammā - O.G. amma - Skt. amba.
kāṇ - OE. con - cna (metathesis) - E.know,
OHG. cna, L. and Gk. gno - Skt. Jna (Ph).
muḻuku - L. merg, Skt. majj.
irum(pu) - O.E.iren. O.G. Mod Ger. eise, Goth. eisarn.
	L. aer. aes; Skt. ayas.
val - L. val Skt. bal.
cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ kīḻiyāriyattiṉum mēliyāriyattil tirintiruppatu, vaṭatiraviṭam vaṭamēṟkāyc ceṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāyt tirintu airōppāvil muḻuvāriyamāy māṟiyatiyum, kīḻiyāriyam intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉ vaṭintiya vaṭivattic cila coṟkaḷil mēṟkoṇṭatiyum uṇarttum.
e-ṭu:
umpar - va.ti. ūppar, Teut. upper, over; L. super, Gk. huper. Skt. upari.
āyiram. ayir - (ayiram) - aciram). North Indian. hazar, Persian. hazar Gk. kesloi; kilioi; Skt. sahasra (sa+hasra).
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ tiyūttāṉiyattiṉum ilattīṉil tirin tiruppatu, vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟa tiraviṭam airōppāviṉ vaṭamēl     mūliyi muṭṭit tirumpiyatiyum, tirumpukālil kiḻakku nōkkit tirintatiyum kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
turuva - Teut. through, L. trans.
tuḷ - turu - turuvu - turuva (vi.e.).
turuttu - Teut. thrust, L. trud (o) (intrude, protrude, obtrude etc).
2. vētavāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
vētavāriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu (ki.mu.2000 - 1500), aṅku vāḻntavaruṭ perumpālār tiraviṭarē. peluccittāṉattilum vaṅkāḷattilum iṉṟum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatiyum, kāḷik kōṭṭam (Calcutta) eṉṉum peyar tūya tamiḻc collā yiruppatiyum, ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
āriya vētam vaṭatiraviṭam vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭil iyaṟṟap peṟṟamiyāl, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ atiṟ pukuntuviṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: akavu, avi - cavi, ilakku, uttaram, uru - uruvu - uruvam, ulaku, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, kaṭukam, kālam, karuḷ (karumi), kumaraṉ - kumari, takkaṇam, taṇṭam, tūtu, naṭam, pakkam, maṇṭalam, mantiram, muttu - muttam, vaṭṭam.
virivañci ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkē iṅku mūlam viḷakkappeṟum.
amalutal = neruṅkutal. amartal = neruṅkutal. amital = neruṅkutal, niṟital, poruntutal, cērtal, kūṭutal. tiraḷutal.         ami = tiraṇṭa keṭṭi mūṅkil.
ami - avi = kūṭṭam. ma-va, pōli.
o.nō: ammi - avvi, kumi - kuvi.
avi - cavi. uyir mutaṟ coṟkaḷ collākkattil moḻi mutal meykaḷ ēṟappeṟum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cakaram.
e-ṭu :	uruḷ - curuḷ, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, ēṇ - cēṇ, ēmam - cēmam, capā (Sabha) eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyiṟ poruttamāṉa vērilli.
uttaram = uyaram, uyaramāṉa vaṭatici. takkaṇam = tāḻvu, tāḻvāṉa teṉtici, kumarimali muḻukip paṉimali tōṉṟiyapiṉ, nāvalattil vaṭatici yuyarntu teṉṟici tāḻntatu. takkut toṇṭi = tāḻnta kural. kuṭamaliyāl uyarntiruppatu mēṟṟiciyeṉṟum, kuṇakaṭalāl tāḻntiruppatu kīḻttici yeṉṟum vaḻaṅki varutal kāṇka.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal. uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, vaṭivuṭip poruḷ. uru - uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumi vaṭivam, atik kāṭṭum iṭiccol. o.nō: aḷavu - aḷapu. uruvu - uruvam. tamiḻilakkaṇam āriya varukikku eṇṇāyiramāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatu.
ulavutal = vaḷital, cuṟṟutal. ulā = nakaric cuṟṟi varum pavaṉi. ulakki = uruṇṭa perumpiṭi. ulamvā - ulamā. ulamarutal = cuḻalutal. ulavu - ulaku = uruṇṭiyāyiruppatu allatu cuṟṟi varuvatu. o.nō: koṭkutal = cuṟṟi varutal. koḷ - kōḷ. ulaku - ulakam. lōk eṉṉum vaṭacol, pārttatu allatu pārkkappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. atu nōkku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripām.
iṉi, ulakam vaṭṭamāyirukkum ñālam (pūmi) eṉṟumām. o.nō: ``kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam'' (kuṟun. 300).
ukaram uyarattik kuṟikkum vērccol. e-ṭu: ukkam = tali, uvvi = tali, utti = oruvakit taliyaṇi. ucci, umpar, uvaṇ, uyar.
uvaṇ = mēliṭam. uvaṇi = tēvarulakam. uvaṇam = uyarcci, paruntu, kaluḻaṉ, kaḻuku. paruntu uyarap paṟakkum paṟavikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ``cuîau¥ gwªjhY« C®¡FUÉ gUªjhFkh?'' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. uvaṇam - cuvaṇam = paruntu, kaluḻaṉ. kaḻuku. uvaṇam (cuvaṇam) mulli nilattup paṟaviyātalāl, an nilatteyvamākiya māyōṉukku ūrtiyākak kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
cuvaṇam eṉṉum colli vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cuparṇa eṉṟu tirittup piṉpu cu + parṇa eṉṟu pirittu, nalla ili, aḻakiya iliyuḷḷatu, aḻakiya ili pōṉṟa ciṟakiyuṭiyatu, eṉṟu poruntāp puḷukalākak karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar. kaṭukaḷavēṉum pakuttaṟi vuṭiyār, nakiccuviyāṉa ic coṟporuṭ karaṇiyattik kāṇiṉ nakātirār.
kummutal = tiraḷutal. kum - kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, iḷiñaṉ. kumari = tiraṇṭavaḷ, iḷiñi. o.nō: L. virginem, E. virgin from virgo to swell. viṭittal = paruttal. vilaṅkiṉattilum paṟavi yiṉattilum tiraṇṭu iḷimiyā yiruppavaṟṟi viṭiyeṉpatu marapu. āṭu, māṭu, kutiri, māṉ, pūṉi, kōḻi, pāmpu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ, tiraṇṭu iḷamiyāyiruppavaṟṟi viṭiyeṉpatu iruvaki vaḻakkilumuṇmi kaṇṭukoḷka. viṭi - viṭali = iḷaṅkāḷi, maṟavaṉ (vīraṉ), iḷiñaṉ, 16 mutal 30 āṇṭu varippaṭṭavaṉ, tiṇṇiyōṉ. iḷanīri viṭaliyeṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku. uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum āṇ paruttiruppatāl, viṭiyeṉpatu iḷavāṇiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. kumar eṉṉum col uyartiṇiyil irupāṟ potuvām. kumari eṉpatu, iḷamipaṟṟic cila marappeyarkkum, putumi (mutaṉmi) kuṟittuc cila toḻiṟpeyarkkum, aḻiyāmi kuṟittuc cila iṭappeyar poruṭ peyarkkum aṭimoḻiyām.
kumaraṉ, kumari eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷi, vaṭavar kumāraṉ kumāri eṉa nīṭṭi, muṟiyē tamiḻp poruḷoṭu makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷilum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉar.
muḷ - muṇṭu - muṇṭi = muṭṭi. muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭi.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kāli maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam. vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaḷittal.
maṇṭalam eṉpatu irukkuvētap perumpiriviṉ peyar. tamiḻc ceyyuḷil ellāvaṭikaḷum aḷavottuvarum yāppu maṇṭilam eṉappaṭum.
``ottā ḻiciyum maṇṭila yāppum
kuṭṭamum nēraṭik koṭṭiṉa eṉpa''	(cey. 114)
``maṇṭilam kuṭṭam eṉṟivi yiraṇṭum
centūk kiyala eṉmaṉār pulavar''	(cey. 116)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ. ``eṉpa'', ``eṉmaṉār pulavar'' eṉṉum vaḻinūṉ marapurikaḷ, `maṇṭilam' eṉṉuñ col mutaṉūṟ kuṟiyī ṭeṉpatit terivikkum. mutaṉūl āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭateṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
maṇṭalam, maṇṭilam eṉṉum irucollum vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ kaḷiyum, tōṟṟattiyum celaviyum kālattiyum iṭattiyum toṉṟutoṭṭu iruvaki vaḻakkilum kuṟittu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. nāṟpatu allatu nāṟpattintu nāṭ koṇṭatu maruttuva nūl kūṟum kāla maṇṭalam. toṇṭi maṇṭalam, koṅku maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa paḻimi yāṉa iṭamaṇṭalam. ``maṇṭilat tarumiyum'' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (977).
taḷ - tāḷ = marañ ceṭi koṭi payir pulliṉ taṭitta aṭi; ili pūviṉ aṭi tāḷi oṭṭiya payir pullili; tāḷ pōṉṟa kāl, viḷakkuttaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa.
taḷ - taṇṭu = ceṭikoṭikaḷiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi. (e-ṭu: kīrittaṇṭu), taṇṭu pōṉṟa poruḷ (e-ṭu: viḷakkuttaṇṭu).
taṇṭu - taṇṭāṉ = taṇṭukkōri. taṇṭutal = taṇṭupōl tiraṭṭutal, paṇattit tiraṭṭutal.
taṇṭu = taṭi, paṭaku valikkuṅ kōl, taṇṭiṉāl tāṅkum pallakku, taṇṭupōl tiraṇṭa paṭi.
o.nō: club = stick association. staff = stick, body of officers.
taṇṭu - taṇṭaṉ = kōl.
taḷ - taṭṭu, taṭṭi = cōḷap payir pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi.
taṇṭu - taṇṭi = parumaṉ mikuti, taṭi, vīṇik kōṭu, taṭitta mūṅkil tāṅkum pallakku. taṇṭi - taṇṭiyam = tāṅkum kaṭṭi. taṇṭi - taṇṭiki = pallakku. taṇṭi - taṇṭiyal = taṇṭiki.
taṇṭittal = paruttal, taṭiyāl aṭittu oṟuttal, oṟuttal.
taṇṭi - taṭi. o.nō: piṇṭi - piṭi, kaṇṭi - kaṭi, maṇṭi - maṭi, 
aṇṭi - aṭi. itu tokuttal tiripu.
taṭittal = paruttal. taṭiyaṉ = paruttavaṉ, parutta pūcaṇikkāy.
taṭi - taṭippu - taṭippam. taṭi - taṭimpu - taṭimpal.
taṇṭu - taṇṭam = taṭi; paṭi. taṇṭattalivaṉ = paṭittalivaṉ.
taṇṭi + am = taṇṭam (oṟuppu). taṇṭi + aṉam = taṇṭaṉam (oṟuppu). taṇṭi + aṉi = taṇṭaṉi (oṟuppu).
taṇṭā eṉpatu kalveṭṭuk kuṟikkum mūvakit taṇṭaṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
taḷ eṉṉum vērccoṟkē paruttaṟ poruḷuḷatu.
taḷ - taḷam = kaṉam, tiṇmi (cīvaka. 719, uri).
taḷam - taṭam = parumi. ``taṭavum kayavum naḷiyum perumi''. (tol. 803).
taṭā = parumi. (kallāṭam, 8 : 15), perumpāṉi (tivā.).
taṭavuttāḻi = peruñcāṭi.
taḷ - tāḷ - tāḷi = kūntaṟpaṉi. tāḷi = tāḻi = perumpāṉi. 
tāḷ - tāṭṭi = parumi, valimi.
`taḻaṅku' eṉṉum col `muḻaṅku' eṉpatupōl olip perumiyi uṇarttum.
muṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal.
muṉṉam = maṉam (tivā.). muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam.
muṉ - maṉ. maṉ + tiram = (maṉṟiram) - mantiram.
o.nō: maṉ - maṉṟu - mantu - manti.
muṉṟu - muntu, piṉṟu - pintu.
mantiram = iṉṉatu ākaveṉṟu uṟutiyāy eṇṇutal, aṅṅaṉam eṇṇic collum uri.
``niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa''	(1434)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpāviyum,
``eṇṇiya veṇṇiyāṅ keytupa veṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ''	(kuṟaḷ. 666)
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiyum, tirumūlar tirumantirattiyum mantiram collic cavittuviṭṭāṉ, mantirattiṉāl vāyik kaṭṭiviṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷiyum; mantiri, mantiram (cūḻviṉi) eṉṉum araciyaṟ coṟkaḷiyum, kūrntu nōkkuka.
vāymoḻi eṉpatu mantirattiṉ maṟupeyar. nālāyirat teyvap paṉuvalil nammāḻvār aruḷicceyta mutalāyiram tiruvāy moḻi yeṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
ākavē, mantiram eṉṉum col tamiḻil araciyal, mataviyal, ilakkaṇam ākiya muttuṟikkuriyatākum. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya varacukaḷ varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāt toṉmi vāyntavi. paṇṭināḷiṟ kiḻakkattiya nāṭukaṭkut tamiḻnāṭṭoṭu araciyal toṭarpum vaṇikattoṭarpum iruntatiṉāl, mantiri eṉṉum col malēyā cīṉam mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum paraviyirukkiṉṟatu. Mandarin eṉṉum āṅkilaccol pōrttukīciya vāyilāyp piṟkālattiṟ pukuntatākum. ataṟkumuṉ mēliyāriyattil ic col illi.
camaṟkiruta iru paṭikaḷ
1. aṭippaṭi - vēta āriyam
2. mēṟpaṭi	1) vaṭa tiraviṭam (pirākirutam)
		2) teṉ tiraviṭam (tamiḻoḻinta pañca tiraviṭam)
		3) tamiḻ
pirākirutam eṉpatu. vētak kālattil vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅkiya vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷ. pirākirutam = muntic ceyyappaṭṭatu. camaṟkirutam eṉpatu, vētamoḻi yilakkaṇa vamitiyip perumpālum taḻuvi, am moḻiyoṭu pirākirutattiyum tiraviṭattiyum (teṉ tiraviṭam) tamiḻiyum kalantu ceyta aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭimoḻi yākum. camaṟkirutam = naṉṟāyc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
pirākirutam eṉṉum col pākatam eṉṟum, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum col caṅkatam, caṉukkirakam allatu cāṉukkirakam eṉṟum, tiriyum.
pirākirutam camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyateṉpatu ataṉ peyarālum moḻiyālum teḷivāyt teriyiṉum, camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟu pirākirutam tirintatākap pētimi mikka paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiyum ārāycciyillāta ayalāriyum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar mayakki varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam), camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ tirintum tiriyāmalum pākatattil vaḻaṅkuvatē.
e-kā:	tiriyātavi		tirintavi
	caṣṭi (sashti)	caṭṭi (satihi)
	tarma (dharma)	tamma (dhamma)
tirintu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭāntam (truṣṭānta), cōttam (tōtra) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ pōṉṟaviyē. avi yāvum aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷalla. pirākirutattiṟkum aṭippaṭi tamiḻē āyiṉum, atu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟātavāṟu attuṇi vaṭa coṟkaḷ atiṟ kalantuḷḷaṉa. vaṭacoṟ kalappu tamiḻil iruppativiṭat tiraviṭattil mikutiyākavum, tiraviṭattil iruppativiṭap pākatattil mikutiyākavum, irukkum. vaṭakkē cellac cella vaṭacoṟ kalappu mikum eṉa aṟika. vēta āriya moḻi vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉataṉāl, atu paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷiṟ kalantu pulluruviyum uṇṇiyum pēṉum pōl mēluṇṇiyāy (Parasite) iruntu varukiṉṟatu. āriyac col vērūṉṟiyavuṭaṉ ataṟku nērāṉa teṉcol vaḻakkaṟṟup pōm. vaḻakkaṟṟa col ilakkiyattil iṭam peṟāviṭiṉ maṟintu mīḷā niliyaṭiyum.
pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭatiraviṭattiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ perumpālum īṟu tirintē vaḻaṅkum.

camaṟkirutat tiraviṭac col muntiya kaṭṭuriyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
camaṟkirutat tamiḻc coṟkaḷ
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷellām, kīḻppaṭiyum mēṟpaṭiyum paṟṟi,
	1) paḻañcol,
	2) putuccol eṉṟum;
moḻi vaki paṟṟi,
	1) āriyac col
	2) tamiḻc col eṉṟum;
vērp poruṭ karaṇiyam uṇmiyum iṉmiyum paṟṟi,
	1) karaṇiyak kuṟi
	2) iṭukuṟi eṉṟum;
ivviru pālāyp pakukkappaṭum.
paḻañcol eṉpaṉa vēta āriyam. iṭukuṟikaḷ `āṉanta piravi', `kaliyāṇi', `caṅkarāparaṇam', `taṉṉiyāci', `nāta nāmak kiriyi', mutaliya paṇṇup peyarkaḷ pōṉṟavi.
tamiḻc coṟkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu mūvakiyāy vakukkap peṟum.
(1) muḻuccol :	e-ṭu :	akavu, tūtu.
(2) vērccol :	e-ṭu :	paṇ - pāṇi (ki), maṉ - manu.

allutal = poruntutal, poruttutal, piṉṉutal, muṭital.
al - aṉ - aṉu = kūṭa.
maṉ - māṉ - māṉi - māṉam = aḷavu, paṭi, matippu, karuttu, perumi.
maṉṉutal = poruntutal.
māṉutal = ottal.
māṉittal = oppiṭṭaḷattal allatu poruttiyaḷattal.

kum - kumpu - kumpam = kuvinta kalam

ā eṉpatu ōr etirmaṟi yiṭiccol. tā = koṭu.
ātā = eṭu.
avamāṉam, aṉumāṉam, upamāṉam, caṉmāṉam, nirmāṇam, piramāṇam, parimāṇam mutaliya pala coṟkaḷ māṉam eṉṉum tamiḻc colli varumoḻiyākayum īṟākavuṅ koṇṭavi.
